Abstract: Runtime traces can appear transparent, but a closed-loop policy determines which states are visited and which failures become visible. We study a simulated hotel-pricing agent mapping time, inventory, and market state to discrete price actions under varying demand regimes. A fault may leave no aggregate trace when the policy rarely visits affected cells. We treat entry into aggregate-only fault interpretation as a diagnosability decision preceding scoring or localization. A reference-map gate requires repeated clean-policy support; a matched runtime gate then requires joint support in clean and current streams. Signal analysis occurs only after both pass. We calibrate false admission on a disjoint clean stream at the physical-component level and model detection by affected clean traffic rather than nominal cell coverage. In a frozen one-shot heldout, 55/72 (76.4%) regime-component units were reference-admitted, representing 20 physical components; 54/55 passed matched runtime admission, while the rejected unit abstained. Stable false admission was 0/20, with a one-sided exact 95% upper bound of 0.1391, meeting the frozen 0.20 criterion. Across 540 repeated unit-arm rows nested in those 20 clusters, affected clean traffic reduced negative log likelihood by 29.3% relative to cell coverage, a gain of 0.1264 nats per row (cluster-bootstrap 95% interval [0.0593, 0.1918]). Adding mask family and its interaction improved log loss by 0.0015 nats per row (one-sided upper bound 0.0066), below the frozen 0.01 practical-sufficiency margin. A development audit found that exact minimum hitting set and greedy selection chose identical supports in 12/12 scenarios because singleton evidence had resolved the conflicts. The result is a bounded rule for interpreting aggregate agent behavior: first establish exposure, then score change, and abstain when the trace cannot support the claim.

</details>


### [26] [Toolcompass: Guiding Tool Trialing, Not Suppressing It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25678)
*Fang, Junlin,Zhang, Chong,Nguyen-Thanh, Do,Xu, Xiaogang,Fang, Zhen,Du, Sean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolCompass通过将工具调用表示按共享功能组织为von Mises-Fisher分布，联合优化类内紧凑与类间分离，引导LLM智能体在部署时对未见工具进行选择性试用。该方法无需真实调用轨迹或未见工具访问，且无推理开销，在AppWorld和FTRL上结合GRPO、RFT、DMPO均取得一致提升，AppWorld OOD任务成功率比原始后训练最高提升10.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在部署时需要泛化到训练中未见过的工具，面临工具试用的两难：过度试用浪费交互预算，而选择性试用才能探索不熟悉的工具；现有基于结果的后训练无法引导浪费性的试用，而回合级监督可能抑制必要的探索。

Method: ToolCompass是一个后训练框架，用于引导LLM智能体的工具试用行为。输入为工具调用表示，输出为经过结构化组织的表示空间。核心模块包括：1）功能类分布建模：将每个功能类（共享功能的工具集合）建模为von Mises-Fisher分布，在表示空间中对齐功能相似的调用；2）联合优化目标：在训练中同时最小化功能类内跨域差异（intra-function variation）并最大化功能类间分离（inter-function separation），从而将已见工具的经验迁移到功能相似的未见工具，引导探索远离无关替代品。训练过程中不需要真实调用轨迹或访问未见工具；推理阶段不引入额外计算开销。该方法可与GRPO、RFT、DMPO等后训练算法结合使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AppWorld OOD task success", "value": "up to 10.71 percentage points", "setting": "over vanilla post-training"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每个功能类建模为von Mises-Fisher分布，按共享功能组织工具调用表示；联合优化目标：减少跨域的功能类内变化，同时增大功能类间分离；无需真实调用轨迹或未见工具访问的后训练框架，且不引入推理开销

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must generalize from tools seen during training to unseen tools at deployment. A key challenge is tool trialing, i.e., excessive trials waste the interaction budget, whereas selective trials enable exploration of unfamiliar tools. Existing outcome-based post-training leaves wasteful trials unguided, while turn-level supervision may suppress necessary exploration. We introduce ToolCompass, a post-training framework that guides tool trialing by organizing tool-call representations according to shared functions. Specifically, ToolCompass models each function class as a von Mises--Fisher distribution and jointly reduces intra-function variation across domains and increases inter-function separation. This structure transfers experience from seen tools to functionally similar unseen tools, directing exploration away from unrelated alternatives. ToolCompass requires no ground-truth call traces or unseen-tool access and incurs no inference overhead. Experiments on AppWorld and FTRL show consistent gains across GRPO, RFT, and DMPO. improves AppWorld OOD task success by up to 10.71 percentage points over vanilla post-training and performs best among competitive baselines on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [27] [Ladders of Thought: A Self-Evolving Curriculum of Progressively Simplified Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25643)
*Liu, Minghui,Magelinski, Thomas,Yuan, Dehao,Yu, Qi,Huang, Furong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoT提出渐进式问题重写与自进化bandit课程调度相结合的LLM推理蒸馏框架，在1-8B模型上于数学和多跳推理任务均显著超越标准知识蒸馏，并收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小规模LLM（1-8B）即使在知识蒸馏下推理能力仍然脆弱，如何通过更有效的训练数据组织与课程调度提升其推理能力。

Method: LoT为训练框架，非单一模型架构。输入为原始推理问题（数学、多跳推理），首先通过渐进式问题重写模块生成语义忠实但简化的变体，保留推理链结构但降低难度；随后用基于步骤数的难度度量将这些变体分入不同难度桶；训练时采用自进化bandit调度器，根据模型当前在各难度桶上的表现自适应分配采样权重，动态推进课程。训练目标仍为知识蒸馏（以强教师模型输出监督），但数据难度与采样比例由bandit在线调整，使模型从易到难逐步学习。推理阶段与标准蒸馏模型一致：直接输入问题生成答案，无额外模块。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "+32pp", "setting": "AddSub算术任务，1-8B模型，对比知识蒸馏基线"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "+25pp", "setting": "SVAMP算术任务，1-8B模型，对比知识蒸馏基线"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "+2-8pp", "setting": "域内测试集，对比知识蒸馏基线"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "+16pp", "setting": "QASC多跳推理数据集"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "+25pp", "setting": "StrategyQA多跳推理数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 渐进式问题重写：自动生成语义忠实但更简单的推理问题变体；基于步骤的难度分桶机制，将重写问题按难度组织；自进化bandit调度器，自适应分配训练数据/难度比例；相比固定分阶段课程收敛更快

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at reasoning when scaled to hundreds of billions of parameters, but small- and mid-scale models remain brittle reasoners even with knowledge distillation (KD). We present Ladders-of-Thought (LoT), a framework that improves reasoning by combining progressive question rewrites with a self-evolving curriculum. LoT automatically generates semantically faithful but easier variants of reasoning problems, organizes them into difficulty buckets using step-based measures, and employs a self-evolving bandit scheduler to allocate training adaptively. Evaluated on two reasoning domains, math and multi-hop reasoning, across 1-8B models from different families, LoT consistently improves over KD. It delivers large gains on arithmetic tasks (e.g., +32 percentage points on AddSub, +25pp on SVAMP), +2-8pp improvements on in-domain test splits, and strong though dataset-dependent benefits on multi-hop reasoning (e.g., +16pp on QASC, +25pp on StrategyQA). LoT also converges faster than staged curricula, highlighting the value of adaptive progression. These results show that progressive rewrites coupled with adaptive curricula provide a simple yet effective recipe for strengthening reasoning in smaller LLMs.

</details>


### [28] [Reasoning-Preserving Fine-Tuning of Post-RL LLMs with Null-Basis LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25618)
*Fang, Wenzhi,Tzou, Nicholas,Valkov, Lazar,Chappidi, Srinivas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 NB-LoRA，通过从推理激活构造零基并重参数化 LoRA 更新，在后 RL 大模型微调中有效保持推理能力，适配性能与标准 LoRA 相当，且推理准确率接近微调前水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对经过强化学习后训练的大语言模型进行后续监督微调时，会严重覆盖已获得的推理能力，现有缓解遗忘的方法要么保留能力有限，要么带来大量训练开销。

Method: NB-LoRA 方法将推理保持问题形式化为逐层的隐藏状态保持约束，并利用推理激活构造一个固定的近似零基。在微调过程中，LoRA 更新通过该零基进行重参数化，使得参数更新落在近似零空间内，从而在适配新领域的同时不干扰原推理能力。训练目标是在标准微调损失基础上，隐式满足该保持约束。推理时，模型结构与标准 LoRA 一致，无额外推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "适配性能", "value": "匹配标准 LoRA", "setting": "多个 RL 训练 LLM 和多样下游任务"}, {"metric": "推理准确率", "value": "保持接近微调前水平", "setting": "多个 RL 训练 LLM 和多样下游任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现推理激活集中在低维子空间，可基于少量样本可靠估计近似零空间，为适配保留容量；提出 NB-LoRA：从推理激活构造固定的近似零基，对 LoRA 更新进行重参数化，从而在微调中强制层级隐藏状态保持约束

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL)-based post-training has become an effective approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs). However, adapting post-RL models to new knowledge domains or behaviors through subsequent supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can severely overwrite these capabilities. Existing approaches mitigate such forgetting through experience replay, specialized initialization, or constrained optimization using gradient projection, but either provide limited preservation or incur substantial training overhead. Our analysis shows that reasoning activations concentrate in low-dimensional subspaces, leaving substantial null-space capacity for adaptation, and that the corresponding approximate null spaces can be reliably estimated from a modest number of examples. Motivated by these observations, we propose Null-Basis Low-Rank Adaptation (NB-LoRA), a parameter-efficient method for adapting post-RL LLMs while preserving their acquired reasoning ability. We formulate reasoning retention as a layer-wise hidden-state preservation constraint and construct a fixed approximate null basis from reasoning activations. LoRA updates are then reparameterized through this basis, enforcing the preservation constraint throughout fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across multiple RL-trained LLMs and diverse downstream tasks show that NB-LoRA matches standard LoRA in adaptation performance, maintains reasoning accuracy near pre-fine-tuning levels, and generalizes this preservation to held-out reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [29] [A Behavioral Trait Leaks into Preferences: Diagnosing Trait Interference in LLM User Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25572)
*Kim, Chaehyun,Kim, Sein,Kang, Hongseok,Park, Chanyoung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现LLM用户模拟器中行为活跃度特质会扭曲偏好特质，导致评估偏向活跃度分布而非推荐性能。提出PQA方法，通过个性化偏好锚点实现页面级质量检查与主动退出，使活跃度仅调节偏好一致页面的浏览深度，从而提高模拟器评估在活跃度变化下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用户模拟器中，注入的行为活跃度特质会干扰偏好特质的表达，导致模型与不匹配物品交互以维持浏览，并使满意度评分因活跃度驱动的页面计数而膨胀，从而偏向特质分布而非推荐器性能，使评估失效。

Method: PQA方法在LLM用户模拟器浏览过程中增加页面级质量锚定模块。对于每个用户，首先基于其偏好属性生成一个个性化的内在偏好标准（锚点）。在模拟浏览时，每当用户到达新页面，模拟器先评估该页面内容是否满足这个个性化锚点；如果不满足，则触发主动退出，不再继续浏览该页面，从而避免因活跃度特质而被迫与不匹配物品交互。只有当页面满足偏好标准时，行为活跃度特质才被用于调节用户在该页面上的浏览深度（如浏览页数）。这种两阶段决策机制将偏好判断与行为调节解耦，原始模拟器中的偏好边界不再被活跃度放大所扭曲，评估分数也更真实反映推荐质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Trait interference mitigation", "value": "定性改善", "setting": "实验显示PQA缓解了特质干扰并提高了模拟器评估在活跃度变化下的可靠性"}, {"metric": "Evaluation reliability", "value": "定性改善", "setting": "同上实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM用户模拟器中行为特质与偏好特质之间的干扰现象（Trait Interference）及其导致的评估无效性（Evaluation Invalidity）；提出了页面级质量锚定方法（PQA），通过个性化锚点指导模拟器评估每页是否满足用户内在偏好标准，从而在低质量页面主动退出，保持活跃度特质仅调节偏好一致页面的浏览深度

Abstract: LLM-based user simulators aim to bridge the offline-online gap in recommender evaluation by emulating users through injected traits, where preference attributes determine what a user engages with and a behavioral activity trait governs how long they browse. However, we show this intended trait independence collapses during simulation, causing two failures: (i) Trait Interference, where amplified activity distorts preference boundaries and forces interactions with mismatched items to sustain browsing, and (ii) Evaluation Invalidity, where satisfaction scores inflate with activity-driven page counts despite taste mismatches, biasing evaluation toward trait distributions rather than recommender performance. To resolve this, we propose PQA, a page-level quality anchoring method that guides simulators using a personalized anchor reflecting each user's intrinsic preference standard. By assessing whether a page meets this standard before further browsing, PQA enables proactive exits from low-quality pages, letting the activity trait retain its intended role of modulating browsing depth within preference-conforming pages. Experiments show PQA mitigates trait interference and improves the reliability of LLM-based simulator evaluation under activity shifts. Our code is available at https://github.com/chaehyun1/PQA

</details>


### [30] [Recovering Agentic Sovereignty: Mitigating the Consensus Paradox via Contrastive Epistemic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25570)
*Shehata, Dahlia,Li, Ming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对 LLM 对群体共识的谄媚/从众问题，提出零样本推理干预 CED，通过单模型双前向对比、非对称概率钳位和 top-k 掩码抑制共识 token，在多个基准和模型上显著降低认知懒惰并恢复准确率，无需微调。对推荐系统算法工程师而言，方法可启发 LLM 推理鲁棒性提升，但与推荐系统直接相关性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 对对抗性群体共识存在参数化脆弱性（从众偏差/谄媚），导致任务性能下降，且无需微调即可缓解。

Method: CED 在标准自回归解码循环中操作。输入为提示词和上下文，主模型进行两次前向传播：一次在原始提示下，一次在带有模拟对抗群体共识的扰动提示下。对比两次前向传播得到的 logits 分布，通过非对称、零界概率钳位和离散 top-k 截断掩码来抑制由群体共识导致的 token 概率提升。具体地，计算两个分布的差异，仅当扰动提示下的 token 概率显著高于原始提示且属于 top-k 时，对该 token 概率进行钳位，防止其占据主导。最终采样时使用修正后的概率分布生成下一个 token。训练目标无需微调，直接在推理时应用。整个流程在单模型上完成，不依赖辅助较弱模型（与标准对比解码不同），因此推理开销增加约一倍前向传播。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "认知懒惰降低 (cognitive loafing reduction)", "value": "up to 33.00% absolute", "setting": "在 GAIA, SWE-bench, Multi-Challenge 基准上，使用 Gemma-2 (9B), Llama-3.1 (8B), Mistral v0.3 (7B) 模型"}, {"metric": "准确率恢复 (accuracy recovery)", "value": "up to 30.75%", "setting": "同上基准和模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Contrastive Epistemic Decoding (CED)，一种零样本推理干预方法，使用同一模型的双前向传播对比来隔离 conformity bias；引入非对称、零界概率钳位（asymmetric, zero-bounded probability clamp）；引入离散 top-k 截断掩码（discrete top-k truncation mask），数学上抑制有毒共识 token 而不引起语法崩溃；展示 CED 解耦合规性与能力，无需微调

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit a parametric vulnerability to adversarial swarm consensus. To mitigate this sycophancy, we introduce Contrastive Epistemic Decoding (CED), a zero-shot inference intervention. Unlike standard Contrastive Decoding (CD) which relies on a weaker secondary model, CED utilizes a dual forward-pass on a single architecture to isolate conformity bias. By introducing a novel asymmetric, zero-bounded probability clamp and discrete top-k truncation mask, CED mathematically suppresses toxic consensus tokens without causing grammatical collapse. Evaluated across 7,200 paired trajectories on complex benchmarks (GAIA, SWE-bench, Multi-Challenge) using Gemma-2 (9B), Llama-3.1 (8B), and Mistral v0.3 (7B), CED successfully neutralizes architectural and positional biases. By reducing cognitive loafing by up to 33.00% absolute, CED drives significant performance gains, yielding up to a 30.75% accuracy recovery. Regaining sovereignty induces distinct architectural behaviors---passive task-focus in Gemma-2 and active refutation of the simulated swarm in Llama-3.1---showing CED decouples compliance from capability without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [31] [ZeroGate: Trust-Preserving Fast Paths for Governed AI Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25443)
*Wang, Zexun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroGate提出受治理AI Agent运行时的快速路径：issuer签短时ActionPass，运行时适配器重建最终动作，本地gate校验绑定并原子消费nonce，以SQLite事务耦合配额与准入回执，从而在授权提前的同时维持决策保持条件。实验在语义fixtures、受控并发/崩溃与Azure Blob的同步vs预准备执行中比较；4800次云端尝试中预准备worker准入到分发p95为9.802–11.374 ms，同步为25.018–334.000 ms，但预准备平均完整生命周期在每个并发水平更长，边界改善并非净速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 把授权提前到动作分发边界之前可以缩短dispatch边界，但可能引入payload、权限或相关状态在批准后发生变化的风险；ZeroGate需要在不取消授权工作的前提下，保证本地成功准入与指定同步策略在准入点授权同一动作，并实现nonce的原子消费与可审计性。

Method: ZeroGate将精确动作审批与耐久本地准入分离。issuer对将要执行的精确动作签发短时ActionPass；可信运行时适配器负责在本地gate处重建最终动作，再由本地gate检查ActionPass与最终动作的绑定关系并消费其nonce。一个SQLite事务将nonce消费、适用的配额更新以及准入回执耦合在一起，使成功准入具有原子性并可审计。论文给出一个条件性决策保持命题：若审批可靠、所有策略依赖都被表示且当前、观测忠实、消费原子，则成功本地准入意味着指定的同步策略会在准入点授权同一动作。实现本身不保证当前世界的新鲜度，也不保证远端效果恰好一次。评估分为作者编写的语义fixtures、受控并发与崩溃实验，以及在Azure Blob上通过同一issuer和gate比较同步执行与预准备执行的研究；两种模式都生成精确动作通行证。生命周期延迟包括准备和预准备批次停留；预准备模式缩短worker准入到分发的边界，但不构成净加速。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "prepared worker-admission-to-dispatch p95", "value": "9.802–11.374 ms", "setting": "4800次云端尝试，不同并发水平；Azure Blob研究，预准备执行"}, {"metric": "synchronous worker-admission-to-dispatch p95", "value": "25.018–334.000 ms", "setting": "4800次云端尝试，不同并发水平；Azure Blob研究，同步执行"}, {"metric": "prepared mean complete lifecycle", "value": "在每个并发水平上均更长", "setting": "4800次云端尝试，不同并发水平；生命周期包含准备与预准备批次停留"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将精确动作审批（issuer签发短时ActionPass）与耐久本地准入分离；可信运行时适配器在本地gate前重建最终动作并检查绑定、消费nonce；SQLite事务原子耦合nonce消费、适用配额更新与准入回执；提出有条件的决策保持命题：在审批可靠、策略依赖完整且当前、观测忠实、消费原子时，本地成功准入等价于指定同步策略在准入点授权同一动作；通过同一issuer和gate对同步执行与预准备执行进行可审计对照，区分准备与批次停留对生命周期延迟的影响；明确实现本身不保证当前世界新鲜度或远端效果恰好一次，指出边界改善不等于净加速

Abstract: Moving authorization earlier can shorten an agent's dispatch boundary without removing authorization work. It can also admit an action whose payload, authority, or relevant state has changed. ZeroGate separates exact-action approval from durable local admission: an issuer signs a short-lived ActionPass, and a trusted runtime adapter reconstructs the final action before a local gate checks its binding and consumes its nonce. A SQLite transaction couples nonce consumption, applicable quota updates, and an admission receipt. We state a conditional decision-preservation proposition: successful local admission implies that a specified synchronous policy would authorize the same action at the admission point, provided approval is sound, all policy dependencies are represented and current, observations are faithful, and consumption is atomic. The implementation alone establishes neither current-world freshness nor exactly-once remote effects. Evaluation separates authored semantic fixtures, controlled concurrency and crash experiments, and an Azure Blob study comparing synchronous and prepared execution through the same issuer and gate. Both modes mint an exact-action pass; lifecycle latency includes preparation and prepared-batch dwell. Across 4800 cloud attempts, prepared worker-admission-to-dispatch p95 ranges from 9.802 to 11.374 ms, versus 25.018 to 334.000 ms synchronously, across the tested concurrency levels. Prepared mean complete lifecycle is longer at every level: the boundary improvement is not a net speedup. The contribution is an explicit revalidation contract, a durable reference boundary, and an auditable comparison of where authorization cost is paid, not a new cryptographic primitive or a universal performance frontier.

</details>


### [32] [Efficient Iterative Retrieval with Heterogeneous Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25405)
*Park, Dohyun,Franke, Hubertus,Waddington, Daniel G.,Sundararaman, Swaminathan,Park, Yongjoo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orthrus 是一个在统一推理循环中实现异构批处理的 LLM 服务系统，通过分块嵌入与增量池化以及工作负载感知的批组成调整，将嵌入模型和生成模型混合批处理，在 4×A100 上相比基线部署提升吞吐 1.28×--4.52×，并将迭代 RAG 端到端 p99 延迟降低最多 55.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务系统将嵌入模型和生成模型隔离执行（如按任务分配 GPU），导致吞吐低、GPU 利用率差，且无法适应动态工作负载，产生计算气泡；核心挑战是在统一推理循环中融合计算模式冲突的嵌入与生成工作负载，并优化批组成。

Method: Orthrus 是一个服务系统，在统一的推理循环中执行嵌入和生成模型的异构批处理。输入是来自动态工作负载的混合推理请求（嵌入请求和生成请求）。系统将这两类请求放入同一个批处理中，以消除因任务隔离导致的计算气泡和 GPU 空闲。为处理嵌入和生成在计算模式上的冲突，Orthrus 采用分块嵌入（chunked embedding）结合增量池化（incremental pooling）：嵌入计算被分块，并在生成解码的间隙中逐步池化，从而更细粒度地交错执行。同时，系统根据当前工作负载动态调整批组成（workload-aware batch composition），以优化吞吐和延迟。该架构在 4 块 A100 GPU 上评估，相比将嵌入和生成模型分开部署的基线方案，在受控工作负载上实现 1.28×--4.52× 的吞吐提升，在迭代 RAG 基准上端到端 p99 延迟降低最高 55.8%。代码已在 GitHub 开源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "吞吐量（throughput）", "value": "1.28×--4.52× higher", "setting": "在4块 A100 GPU 上、受控工作负载（controlled workloads）下，与基线部署相比"}, {"metric": "端到端 p99 延迟（end-to-end p99 latency）", "value": "up to 55.8% lower", "setting": "在迭代 RAG（iterative-RAG）基准测试上"}]}

Conclusion: 在统一推理循环中实现异构批处理（heterogeneous batching），将嵌入和生成请求混合批处理；分块嵌入（chunked embedding）与增量池化（incremental pooling），以协调嵌入与生成的不同计算模式；工作负载感知的批组成调整（workload-aware batch composition adjustment）

Abstract: Modern information retrieval increasingly employs both embedding and generative models to handle complex queries. However, current serving systems suffer from low throughput and poor GPU utilization because they execute these models in isolation. Coarse-grained partitioning, such as dedicating GPUs to specific tasks, fails to adapt to dynamic workloads and creates computational "bubbles". To address these, we present Orthrus, a serving system that performs heterogeneous batching within a unified inference loop. The primary challenge lies in unifying embedding and generation workloads with conflicting computational patterns while optimizing batch composition for high performance. Orthrus addresses these challenges through chunked embedding with incremental pooling and by adjusting batch composition in a workload-aware manner. Evaluation on four A100 GPUs shows that, relative to baseline deployments, Orthrus achieves 1.28$\times$--4.52$\times$ higher throughput on controlled workloads and up to 55.8% lower end-to-end p99 latency on an iterative-RAG benchmark. We release our code at https://github.com/illinoisdata/Orthrus .

</details>


### [33] [Robust Failure, Conservative Repair: Textual Knowledge Distillation from Cross-Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25400)
*Ren, Andrew,Liu, Haokun,Tan, Chenhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RFCR通过从跨模型共享失败中蒸馏规则原子、用边界案例约束应用范围并在无规则时弃权，提升推理准确性，在BIG-Bench Hard 400项上从68.50%升至71.25%且不损害原有正确案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于失败的文本知识蒸馏中，从单一模型失败提取的规则原子可能包含模型特定推理补丁，对其他模型不具迁移性且易被误应用于无关案例，导致知识迁移不稳定。

Method: 论文基于将训练示例蒸馏为任务特定cheat sheets以辅助模型推理的pipeline。输入为模型在任务上的失败案例（跨模型比较）；RFCR首先收集多个模型共同出现的失败，从中提炼规则原子（rule atoms），每个规则原子是可注入模型文本输入的独立推理规则。随后利用边界案例（boundary cases）对规则的应用条件进行锐化，明确其适用/不适用范围，避免误应用于无关案例。最后，当未找到有用规则时进行弃权（abstain），不添加规则。规则原子在推理时注入文本输入，辅助源模型和其他模型推理。核心目标函数/优化：提高任务准确率同时不在原先正确案例上引入性能下降（无退化）。验证：在400项BIG-Bench Hard任务集上评估，与基线cheat sheets对比，并通过消融和跨模型诊断分析增益来源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "68.50% → 71.25% (+2.75 pp; 95% CI [+1.25,+4.50])", "setting": "BIG-Bench Hard 400项任务集，RFCR对比基线cheat sheets"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出RFCR文本蒸馏流程：从多模型共享失败中提取规则、利用边界案例锐化应用边界、无有用规则时弃权；定义规则原子为可注入模型文本输入的独立规则，并区分可迁移任务知识与模型特定推理补丁；通过跨模型诊断和消融验证准确率增益来自新知识注入与严格规则应用控制

Abstract: Failure-based textual knowledge distillation aims to discover gaps in a model's knowledge by examining its task errors. The distilled knowledge can be useful for the reasoning of both this model ("source model") and other models. However, this transfer of knowledge may not be stable. We define a rule atom to be a standalone rule injected into a model's textual input at inference time. A rule atom can encode transferable task knowledge or model-specific reasoning patches that can confuse other models. Also, the injected rule atoms can be misapplied to unrelated cases, causing the model to incorrectly flip its answer based on irrelevant information. Building on a pipeline that distills training examples into task-specific cheat sheets that aid model reasoning, we examine when failure-derived rules can improve these cheat sheets. Our early experiment shows rule distillation from a single model's failures underperforms the baseline cheat sheet on non-source model families. This motivates Robust Failure, Conservative Repair (RFCR), a textual distillation procedure that derives rules from failures shared across models, sharpens their application boundaries using boundary cases, and abstains when no useful rule is found. On a 400-item BIG-Bench Hard task set, RFCR improves the baseline cheat sheets from 68.50% to 71.25% (+2.75 pp; 95% CI [+1.25,+4.50]) without performance degradation on previously correct cases. Ablations and cross-model diagnostics support that accuracy gains come from both new knowledge injection and strict rule-application control.

</details>


### [34] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Torres, Ethan,Mills, Eric*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出潜在等价学习框架，通过身份-实现分离、高斯原型、软隶属和查询条件路由，将企业结构化数据的跨 schema 证据预先组织，供 LLM 代理直接推理。在 Data Agent Benchmark（12 个异构数据集 54 个查询）上达到 94.67% Pass@1，显著高于基准 Claude Opus 4.6 代理的 55.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据代理在每次查询时都需要重新发现跨 schema、关系、政策与业务角色的相关证据，现有 markdown 式记忆/技能文件虽可复用但未将语义推理与结构化预测分工，导致重复结构发现和代理推理负担。

Method: 框架将任务相关身份与其数据集相对实现分离。输入为结构化企业数据环境中的证据，包括支持性和反对性证据。支持/反对证据共同塑造 support-realized Gaussian prototypes，建模持久身份在特定数据环境中的具体表达；soft-membership profiles 对每个样本保留多个原型的隶属关系，避免硬分配丢失区分信息。另一路独立的 learned query-prototype 系统表示重复出现的查询证据需求，并通过 learned compatibility function 映射到相同的持久身份结构。最终进行 identity-factorized、query-conditioned routing：以查询为条件，将相关的数据集特定证据物化/materialize 成已组织的证据状态。下游 LLM 代理直接该状态上执行语义推理，而无需在每次查询时重建跨 schema 的结构。训练目标涉及原型学习、软隶属和兼容性函数的学习，但论文摘要未披露具体损失函数、优化器或训练细节。推理路径为：查询输入 → 查询原型表示 → 兼容性映射到身份结构 → 路由/物化数据集特定证据 → 下游代理推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "dataset-macro stratified Pass@1", "value": "94.67%", "setting": "Data Agent Benchmark, 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, 5 complete trials"}, {"metric": "successful raw query attempts", "value": "258/270", "setting": "Data Agent Benchmark"}, {"metric": "dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 (reference agent)", "value": "55.51%", "setting": "benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 潜在等价学习（latent equivalence learning）：将持久任务相关身份（persistent identities）与其数据集相对实现（dataset-relative realizations）分离；支持/反对证据塑造的 support-realized Gaussian prototypes，学习身份在特定数据环境中的表达；soft-membership profiles 保留硬分配下丢失的区分信息；独立的 learned query-prototype 系统表示重复出现的证据需求，并通过 learned compatibility function 映射到同一持久身份结构；identity-factorized、query-conditioned routing 将数据集特定证据物化，供下游代理在已组织证据状态上推理

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [35] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Manvi, Ash,Tajreen, Samreena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将注意力视为单次前向即可获得的路由图，提取少量指向答案的路由并验证其因果重要性。在归纳与IOI任务上，消融提取边比消融同规模随机边对模型损伤更大；提取成本仅一次前向，比头级补丁扫描快约两个数量级。实验覆盖GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium与Pythia-410M，采用配对间隙检验与自助置信区间，n=100提示/单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何以极低计算代价从单次前向传播中提取携带真实因果信号的注意力路由图，而无需大量干预或头级补丁扫描。

Method: 方法将一次前向传播中的注意力模式视为一张路由图，节点为注意力头或token表示，边为注意力路由。主要流程：对输入提示执行单次前向，收集各层注意力权重，保留少量指向答案或与答案预测高度相关的路由（提取边集）；随后对提取出的边进行消融，并对比随机选取的同规模边集，通过配对间隙检验与自助置信区间判断提取边是否对模型输出具有因果重要性。评估在归纳与IOI两个已知电路任务上进行，覆盖GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium与Pythia-410M，每个条件n=100提示。训练目标不适用（不训练新模型），优化目标为在保持低成本的前提下最大化提取边的因果信号。推理/服务路径：单次前向提取路由草图，后续可选进行消融验证；提取步骤本身即单次前向，无需反向传播或大量干预。论文明确不声称完整电路图谱，而是提供携带真实因果信号的廉价草图，并指出在失败模式下的明确局限。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "消融提取边导致的模型损伤 vs 消融同规模随机边", "value": "提取边损伤显著更大（未报告具体数值）", "setting": "归纳与IOI任务，n=100提示/单元，GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium、Pythia-410M，配对间隙检验与自助置信区间"}, {"metric": "提取步骤计算成本对比头级补丁扫描", "value": "约快两个数量级", "setting": "单次前向提取 vs 头级补丁扫描"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将注意力视为一次前向即可获得的路由图，保留少量指向答案的路由并检验其因果重要性；在已知电路任务（归纳与IOI）上用消融边缘与随机边缘对比验证提取边的实际作用；提出配对间隙检验与自助置信区间，在n=100提示/单元下评估GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium与Pythia-410M；提取步骤仅需一次前向，相比头级补丁扫描约快两个数量级

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter. They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more. We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>

### [36] [SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26780)
*Zheng, Haobo,Tang, Tan,Chen, Yan,Wang, Weijie,Wu, Yingcai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpeakerMem-R1 面向多方对话长期记忆，提出双轨记忆（带说话人标签的逐字消息 + 个人/群组级结构化状态），查询时按实体、事件、时间融合证据；用 SpeakerLevenshtein 与 speaker-conditioned GRPO 训练 Writer-R1 减少归属和更新错误。在 GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench、EverMemBench 与 LoCoMo 上报告 binary accuracy 与 token-F1，消融验证双轨与双视图互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方对话长期记忆中难以区分谁说、涉及谁、个体间感知、群组共享信息与状态随时间变化，核心瓶颈是消息归属/关系理解与交错历史中的状态重建。

Method: SpeakerMem-R1 采用双轨记忆架构。输入为多方长期对话历史；第一轨按说话人标签保存逐字消息，保留原始表述与归属；第二轨将对话派生为结构化状态，并组织为个人级视图（个体的状态/感知）与群组级视图（群组共享信息）。写入阶段由 Writer-R1 完成结构化记忆构建，其训练使用 SpeakerLevenshtein 度量与 speaker-conditioned GRPO，以降低说话人归属错误和状态更新错误，并面向本地部署优化。查询阶段对双轨记忆进行检索，按实体、事件、时间三类线索抽取并融合两轨以及个人级/群组级视图中的证据，用于回答需要跨成员、跨群组、跨时间整合的问题。评测在 GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench、EverMemBench、LoCoMo 及 305 题受控评测上进行，并报告 binary accuracy 与 token-F1，消融显示逐字轨与结构化轨、个人级与群组级视图在标准化评测接口下互补。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "binary accuracy", "value": "47.9%", "setting": "GroupMemBench"}, {"metric": "binary accuracy", "value": "69.2%", "setting": "SocialMemBench"}, {"metric": "binary accuracy", "value": "61.9%", "setting": "EverMemBench"}, {"metric": "binary accuracy", "value": "62.33%", "setting": "公开的 EverMemBench 排行榜（EverMind-AI）报告结果，称在最新 SOTA 框架中最佳"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "70.85%", "setting": "LoCoMo 全部 1,986 个问题（作为两人长期对话边界测试）"}, {"metric": "mean accuracy", "value": "由 57.38% 提升至 68.20%", "setting": "305 个问题的受控评测，RL 相对 SFT Writer"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: SpeakerMem-R1 双轨记忆：一条存储带说话人标签的逐字消息，另一条存储派生的结构化状态，并组织为个人级与群组级视图；查询时按实体、事件、时间三类线索融合双轨与个人/群组视图证据；Writer-R1 训练：使用 SpeakerLevenshtein 与 speaker-conditioned GRPO 降低结构化记忆构建中的归属和更新错误，并支持本地部署；标准化评测接口下的双轨及个人/群组视图消融，验证互补性

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.

</details>


### [37] [CoVeR: Coverage-Based Routing of Verifier Calls in Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26086)
*Roh, Daeyoung,Han, Donghee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoVeR 提出用冻结句嵌入的 coverage margin 单一阈值门控智能体检索中的 verifier 调用，在三个多跳 QA 基准上答案准确率与全验证基线相差不到零点几个 EM 点，同时削减 62-68% 的 verifier 调用（饱和时 93%），并可将路由器蒸馏为 921k 参数头，使路由回路不含 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体检索中，每一步都调用 LLM verifier 判断证据是否足够会带来随检索步数增长的重复验证成本；需要在不显著损失答案准确率的前提下跳过大部分 verifier 调用。

Method: 系统面向 agentic retrieval：agent 迭代发出搜索查询，获得证据后需要决定是否停止。基线做法是每步调用 LLM verifier 或 prompt judge 对不断增长的证据做判断，成本随步数累积。CoVeR 引入一个前置门控：对当前证据计算冻结句嵌入的 coverage margin，并用单一阈值判断证据是否仍然明显不完整；若明显不完整则直接跳过 verifier 继续检索，仅将模糊状态交给 verifier/decider 判定是否停止。路由器可完全由该覆盖信号构成，也可蒸馏为一个 921k 参数的轻量头（drafter）接在冻结编码器上，从而使路由回路无 LLM。工作流为：查询→证据累积→coverage margin 计算→阈值门控→（跳过 or 调用 verifier）→停止或继续检索。论文强调该信号只能用于路由，不能替代验证本身。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "verifier calls reduction", "value": "62-68%", "setting": "三个多跳 QA benchmark，评估协议在全量运行前固定"}, {"metric": "verifier calls reduction", "value": "93%", "setting": "饱和 regime"}, {"metric": "answer accuracy (EM)", "value": "match full-budget agent and always-verify baseline within a fraction of an EM point", "setting": "三个多跳 QA benchmark"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: CoVeR 门控：用冻结句嵌入的 coverage margin 单一阈值识别证据明显不完整的检索状态，仅对模糊状态调用 verifier；证明基于证据计数、词汇重叠、BM25 相关性的路由器（单独或组合学习）整体权衡更差；门控在无需重调的情况下可跨不同 decider 与 agent scale 迁移；将 drafter 蒸馏为冻结编码器之上的 921k 参数头，使路由回路中不含 LLM；明确指出该覆盖信号不能替代验证：匹配一个 claim 比判定该 claim 被支持容易得多

Abstract: An agentic retrieval system issues a sequence of search queries and must decide, at each step, whether the evidence collected so far is enough to stop. Delegating that decision to an LLM verifier or a prompt judge makes stopping reliable, but the verifier then reprocesses the growing evidence after every retrieval step, a substantial repeated cost. We show that most of these calls can be skipped without materially changing answer accuracy: a single threshold on a frozen sentence-embedding coverage margin detects the states in which the evidence is still plainly incomplete, and the verifier is called only on the ambiguous remainder, a gate we call CoVeR (Coverage-based Verifier Routing). Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, with the evaluation protocol fixed before the full-scale run, the CoVeR-gated agent matches the answer accuracy of both the full-budget agent and the always-verify baseline within a fraction of an EM point. It cuts 62-68% of verifier calls, and 93% in a saturated regime. Routers built on evidence counts, lexical overlap, or BM25 relevance, alone or learned in combination, give weaker overall trade-offs, the gate transfers without re-tuning across deciders and agent scales, and its drafter distills into a 921k-parameter head atop the frozen encoder, leaving no LLM in the routing loop. The same signal cannot replace verification: matching a claim is far easier than deciding the claim is supported.

</details>


### [38] [Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26637)
*Luo, Xiaoyu,Ren, Tao,Yu, Wenrui,Li, Xiao,Li, Qiongxiu,Bjerva, Johannes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过标准API注册自定义工具，成功提取闭源前沿模型本应隐藏的链式思维轨迹，并在多种任务上验证其与原生CoT性能相当。进一步从token效率、步骤类型和推理树结构等维度刻画模型推理的组织方式，为理解前沿模型推理行为提供了超越基准分数的行为学视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源前沿模型的原始链式思维（CoT）轨迹被隐藏，即无法验证其推理能力是否真正提升，也因此无法理解模型如何组织中间推理。

Method: 论文提出的方法包含两个主要阶段。第一阶段是推理提取：通过在标准API中注册一个简单的自定义工具，当模型调用该工具时会被诱导输出其中间推理步骤，从而实现原本隐藏的CoT的外化。为了验证这些外部化轨迹是否反映真实推理而非事后合理化，作者将提取的推理与开源模型的原生CoT进行对比，并将实验扩展到闭源前沿模型（包括GPT-6 Astra），在竞赛数学、科学和代码生成任务上评估性能，发现提取推理匹配原生推理且显著优于无推理基线。第二阶段是推理特征刻画：从token效率（外化所需的token数量）、推理步骤类型（如内部解决的基本步骤与关键推理步骤的区分）以及诱导推理树（推理路径的组织结构）三个维度分析模型如何外化、压缩和组织推理。观察到Astra表现出token高效的定向推理：更早选择正确轨迹，内部解决基本步骤，仅外化关键推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过标准API注册简单自定义工具来诱导前沿模型外化中间推理轨迹，从而获取原本隐藏的CoT。；系统性地在竞赛数学、科学和代码生成任务上比较提取推理与原生CoT及无推理基线的性能，验证提取推理的真实性。；从token效率、推理步骤类型和诱导推理树三个维度刻画前沿模型组织推理的结构差异。

Abstract: The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.

</details>


### [39] [Magnitude Profile Pruning: Calibration-Free Structured Attention Head Removal for Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26177)
*Dewage, Kasun,Pensky, Marianna,Rathnasekara, Heranga K.,De Silva, Suranadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Magnitude Profile（MP）评分，一种免训练、免校准的结构化注意力头剪枝准则，基于权重行范数的离群检测判断头重要性，并给出适配GQA的MP-G变体。在OPT-6.7B和RoBERTa-large等五个模型上，12.5%-50%稀疏度下MP-G困惑度优于Wanda-Head、SparseGPT-Head、Gradient-Head等依赖校准的基线；50%稀疏度带来最多16%参数减少和50%注意力FLOP节省，且无需前向传播或梯度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力头重要性度量依赖校准数据、梯度计算或Hessian估计，带来额外开销且依赖数据；需要一种免训练、零成本的结构化头剪枝准则。

Method: 方法作用于Transformer注意力模块。每个注意力头由query、key、value等投影权重矩阵的行向量定义。MP scoring对每个头的投影权重行计算范数，并将所有头的行范数视为一个总体分布，通过统计离群检测判断某个头的范数是否落在群体主体（bulk）内：落在主体内的头被判定为可剪枝，因为其权重范数不具离群性，代表其表示容量相对较小；而范数显著离群的头被保留，认为其承担了不成比例的表示能力。MP-G进一步处理Grouped Query Attention（GQA）：由于多个query头共享同一组key/value投影，先计算共享组的分数，再将其分配到与之关联的各个query头上，以获得每个头的有效重要性评分。整个评分过程仅利用预训练权重，不需要任何前向传播、校准样本或梯度/Hessian计算。确定各头分数后，按目标稀疏度（如12.5%、25%、50%）剪除分数最低（即非离群）的头，得到结构化剪枝后的模型。评估时直接使用剪枝后的模型在WikiText-2上计算困惑度，并统计参数减少比例与注意力FLOP节省。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WikiText-2 perplexity", "value": "18.46", "setting": "OPT-6.7B, 12.5% head sparsity, MP-G"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 perplexity", "value": "27.87", "setting": "OPT-6.7B, 25% head sparsity, MP-G"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 perplexity", "value": "152.0", "setting": "OPT-6.7B, 50% head sparsity, MP-G"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 perplexity", "value": "7.27", "setting": "RoBERTa-large, 12.5% head sparsity, MP-G"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 perplexity", "value": "10.28", "setting": "RoBERTa-large, 25% head sparsity, MP-G"}, {"metric": "parameter reduction", "value": "up to 16%", "setting": "50% head sparsity"}, {"metric": "attention FLOP savings", "value": "50%", "setting": "50% head sparsity"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Magnitude Profile (MP) scoring：基于权重行范数的统计离群检测，将投影权重落在群体分布内（非离群）的注意力头剪除，保留离群头（代表较大表示容量）。；MP-G 变体：针对 Grouped Query Attention (GQA)，将共享的 key-value 组分数分配到关联的 query heads 上进行重要性评分。；完全无需前向传播、校准样本或梯度计算，实现零成本结构化头剪枝。

Abstract: Structured pruning of attention heads provides a hardware-friendly way to compress Transformer language models. However, existing methods for measuring head-level importance require calibration data, gradient computation, or Hessian estimation. These requirements add extra overhead and make the methods depend on the data. Our work presents Magnitude Profile (MP) scoring, a training-free criterion for head importance that identifies dispensable heads through statistical outlier detection on weight row norms. Heads whose projection weights fall within the population bulk are pruned, while heads exhibiting outlier norms, which carry disproportionate representational capacity, are preserved. Our work further gives MP-G, a variant that handles Grouped Query Attention (GQA) by distributing shared key-value group scores across associated query heads. Across five models evaluated on WikiText-2 perplexity at 12.5%-50% head sparsity, MP-G achieves the best perplexity on OPT-6.7B at all sparsity levels (18.46 at 12.5%, 27.87 at 25%, 152.0 at 50%). MP-G also gives the best results on RoBERTa-large at 12.5% and 25% sparsity, with perplexity values of 7.27 and 10.28, outperforming calibration-dependent baselines including Wanda-Head, SparseGPT-Head, and Gradient-Head. It requires zero forward passes, calibration samples, or gradient computation. At 50% sparsity, head pruning yields up to 16% parameter reduction with 50% attention FLOP savings. Our results show that weight-only statistical scoring can match or outperform data-dependent methods for structured head pruning, providing a practical, zero-cost criterion for Transformer compression.

</details>


### [40] [TSS: Target-Side Sparsification for Speculative Decoding in Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26100)
*Hu, Haibo,Huang, Lianming,Li, Qiao,Guan, Nan,Xue, Chun Jason*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TSS提出对投机解码的目标模型侧进行稀疏化：在领域特定推理中跳过部分目标层，用接受率与任务指标感知的宽度搜索选取多层跳过配置，并用轻量级skip controller按领域动态应用。无需重训练或永久剪枝，一个完整目标模型即可支持多条稀疏验证路径。在Spec-Bench上，Translation设置平均接受长度从2.70提升到4.53（+67.8%），BLEU从0.131提升到0.237（+80.9%），端到端吞吐从75.6提升到127.3 tokens/s（1.68X加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在领域特定场景下，投机解码的目标模型验证阶段仍保持全深度稠密计算，验证成本高且未必最优；需要在不重训练、不永久剪枝的前提下降低目标侧验证开销并提升草稿接受率。

Method: TSS面向投机解码的两阶段流程：草稿模型生成候选token序列，目标模型进行验证。其核心改变在目标侧：不再对目标模型全部层做完整前向验证，而是允许跳过目标模型中的若干选定层，形成一条稀疏验证路径。为确定跳过哪些层，TSS采用接受率与任务指标双重感知的宽度搜索（breadth search），在多层跳过配置空间中探索候选配置，且不预设接受率与任务指标之间的固定优先级，从而找到在两者间取得良好折中的配置集合。搜索得到的配置按领域存储为‘领域到配置’映射；推理时由轻量级skip controller根据当前领域选取对应配置并施加于目标模型，跳过指定层。由于跳过是运行时按配置执行的，同一个完整目标模型仍保留全部参数，可同时支持多条稀疏验证路径，无需重新训练或进行永久性参数剪枝。该设计通过降低目标验证的计算深度来减少验证成本，同时因稀疏路径与领域分布匹配而提高草稿token的接受率，进而在下游任务指标（如BLEU）和端到端吞吐上取得提升。论文未披露具体的层选择算法细节、skip controller实现、模型参数量或硬件资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均接受长度（average accept length）", "value": "2.70 → 4.53 (+67.8%)", "setting": "Translation 设置，Spec-Bench 多领域/多模型规模/多投机解码方法的实验"}, {"metric": "BLEU", "value": "0.131 → 0.237 (+80.9%)", "setting": "Translation 设置，Spec-Bench"}, {"metric": "端到端吞吐（end-to-end throughput）", "value": "75.6 → 127.3 tokens/s (1.68X speedup)", "setting": "Translation 设置，Spec-Bench"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现领域特定推理下跳过部分目标层可降低验证成本、同时提升草稿接受率并保持或提升下游任务表现；提出接受率与任务指标感知的宽度搜索（acceptance- and metric-aware breadth search），在不设定两目标固定优先级的情况下探索多层跳过配置；构建领域到配置的映射（domain-to-configuration mapping），并用轻量级skip controller在推理时应用，使同一个完整目标模型支持多条稀疏验证路径，无需重训练或永久参数剪枝

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through collaboration between a lightweight draft model and a target verifier. Existing methods mainly improve the draft side, while the target model is typically kept dense and unchanged. We show that, under domain-specific inference, full-depth target verification is not always the optimal choice. Counter-intuitively, skipping selected target layers can reduce verification cost while simultaneously increasing draft acceptance and preserving, or even improving, downstream task performance. Based on this observation, we propose TSS, a target-side sparsification framework for speculative decoding. TSS employs an acceptance- and metric-aware breadth search to explore multi-layer skip configurations without imposing a fixed priority between the two objectives. The selected configurations are stored in a domain-to-configuration mapping and applied by a lightweight skip controller, allowing one complete target model to support multiple sparse verification paths without retraining or permanent parameter pruning. Experiments on Spec-Bench across multiple domains, model scales, and speculative decoding methods show consistent improvements in draft acceptance and downstream task performance. In Translation setting, TSS increases the average accept length from 2.70 to 4.53 (+67.8%), improves BLEU from 0.131 to 0.237 (+80.9%), and raises end-to-end throughput from 75.6 to 127.3 tokens/s, corresponding to a 1.68X speedup.

</details>


### [41] [Compressing Long Context into Answer-Aligned Memory Embeddings for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25537)
*Rahman, Md Mostafizer,Amin, Md Faizul Ibne,Mia, Md Shahajada,Watanobe, Yutaka,Liu, Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CMC框架，将长上下文压缩为与冻结解码器嵌入空间对齐的Context Memory Embeddings，并设计两层KV cache（问题引导CME选择+局部窗口），通过答案导向蒸馏训练压缩器。在九个编码器-解码器组合和四个QA基准上验证，SQuAD最高提升7.3 EM和4.0 F1，同时推理时间与能耗最多降20%、峰值预留显存最多降50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理受自注意力二次复杂度和KV cache线性增长限制，长上下文导致延迟、能耗和GPU显存需求上升；现有软压缩方法缺少推理时的查询引导记忆选择、未使用答案目标监督、且与特定解码器架构紧耦合。

Method: CMC框架由上下文压缩器、冻结解码器和两层KV cache组成。输入为长上下文与问题。压缩器将长输入上下文编码为紧凑的Context Memory Embeddings（CMEs），并通过训练对齐到任意冻结解码器的嵌入空间。训练阶段采用答案导向蒸馏：以冻结LLM的输出为监督信号，训练压缩器使CMEs保留生成正确答案所需的信息，不更新解码器权重。推理阶段使用两层KV cache：第一层存储问题引导选择后的CMEs，第二层保留局部上下文窗口。生成时，根据当前问题对CMEs进行引导选择，与局部窗口的KV共同参与自回归生成，从而在降低KV cache长度和注意力计算量的同时保持回答质量。该设计分别解决推理时无查询引导记忆选择、训练无答案目标监督、以及与特定解码器架构紧耦合三个问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "EM (Exact Match)", "value": "up to +7.3", "setting": "SQuAD，跨九种编码器-解码器组合与四个QA基准的实验"}, {"metric": "F1", "value": "up to +4.0", "setting": "SQuAD，跨九种编码器-解码器组合与四个QA基准的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将长输入上下文压缩为紧凑的Context Memory Embeddings（CMEs），并使其对齐到任意冻结解码器的嵌入空间，无需修改解码器权重。；设计两层KV cache：问题引导的CME选择机制与局部上下文窗口相结合，在推理时动态选取与问题相关的记忆。；采用来自冻结LLM的答案导向蒸馏来训练压缩器，使压缩表示直接面向答案生成目标进行监督。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is constrained by the quadratic scaling of self-attention and the linear scaling of the KV cache, increasing latency, energy consumption, and GPU memory demand as context length scales. Existing soft-compression methods either lack query-guided memory selection at inference time, train without answer-targeted supervision, or couple compression tightly to a specific decoder architecture. We propose a Context-to-Answer-Aligned Memory Compression (CMC) framework, which compresses long input contexts into compact Context Memory Embeddings (CMEs) aligned to any frozen decoder's embedding space, reducing inference costs without modifying decoder weights. CMC introduces a two-tier KV cache that combines question-guided CME selection with a local context window, and trains the compressor with answer-targeted distillation from a frozen LLM. Experiments across nine encoder-decoder combinations and four QA benchmarks show that CMC consistently outperforms the baseline, achieving up to 7.3 EM and 4.0 F1 point gains on SQuAD, while reducing inference time and energy consumption by up to 20% and peak reserved GPU memory by up to 50% at 3,000 generation tokens. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component and training objective contributes to the performance.

</details>
