*Villani, Simon P.*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次演示了不同大小的混合语言模型之间的持久循环推理状态迁移，无需目标前缀重放。通过翻译注意力 KV 并加入 Gated DeltaNet 状态包，在 PG19 文档上显著降低教师强制 NLL 达 0.747 nats/token，并引入 434,176 参数校正模块使延续损失仅比原生 9B 高 0.076 nats/token。证据限于单向、几何匹配的 Base 模型对和 4K 教师强制延续，近原生门限未通过，16K 分支未运行，自由生成等价性和通用状态接口尚未证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能否让一个语言模型在不重新读取上下文的情况下，将其持久记忆（KV 缓存和循环状态）交接给另一个不同大小的语言模型？

Method: 论文提出 LatentPort 方法，用于在架构匹配的 Qwen3.5 4B 和 9B 兄弟模型之间迁移持久推理状态。流程如下：源模型（4B）在处理完上下文后，其注意力 KV 缓存被翻译为目标模型（9B）的对应形式；同时，源模型的 Gated DeltaNet (GDN) 持久循环状态和卷积状态被直接重用（或通过学习的 GDN 映射转换）。目标模型接收这些迁移状态后，无需重新读取历史前缀即可继续生成。训练目标方面，使用教师强制负对数似然 (NLL) 作为主要评估指标，并引入一个 434,176 参数的校正模块来进一步对齐目标模型的状态。推理时，迁移后的状态与校正模块共同作用，使 9B 模型在零历史前缀令牌的情况下处理延续任务。该方法旨在解决跨模型状态迁移中的兼容性问题，实验表明直接重用循环和卷积状态优于学习的映射，暗示持久状态坐标具有部分功能兼容性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "负对数似然 (NLL)", "value": "降低 0.747 nats/token", "setting": "教师强制，64 篇 PG19 文档，95% 配对文档自助法 CI [0.6921, 0.8047]"}, {"metric": "延续损失超出原生 9B 的量 (excess NLL)", "value": "0.076 nats/token", "setting": "64 篇新网络文档，加入校正模块后"}, {"metric": "Jensen-Shannon 散度", "value": "0.022", "setting": "64 篇新网络文档，加入校正模块后"}, {"metric": "原生上下文恢复 (NCR)", "value": "0.918", "setting": "64 篇新网络文档，加入校正模块后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次演示了跨模型、不同大小的混合语言模型之间的持久循环推理状态迁移，无需目标前缀重放。；通过翻译注意力 KV 并加入 Gated DeltaNet 持久状态包，显著降低教师强制负对数似然。；直接重用循环和卷积状态优于所测试的学习型 GDN 映射，表明持久状态坐标存在部分功能兼容性。；引入一个 434,176 参数的校正模块，进一步改善基础迁移效果。

Abstract: Can one language model hand its live memory to another without the receiver rereading the context? We demonstrate useful persistent hybrid-state transfer across one architecture-matched Qwen3.5 4B-to-9B sibling pair. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstrated cross-model handoff of persistent recurrent inference state between differently sized hybrid language models without target prefix replay. Translated attention KV alone leaves a large gap; adding the Gated DeltaNet (GDN) persistent-state package lowers teacher-forced negative log-likelihood (NLL), the average next-token log-loss, by 0.747 nats/token (95% paired document bootstrap CI [0.6921, 0.8047]), improving all 64 PG19 documents. Direct recurrent and convolution reuse outperforms the tested learned GDN maps, consistent with partial functional compatibility of persistent-state coordinates. A fresh component factorial selects translated KV with direct recurrent and convolution state. An additional 434,176-parameter correction improves that base on 64 fresh web documents: continuation loss is 0.076 nats/token above native 9B (excess NLL), Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence is 0.022, and native context recovery (NCR) is 0.918. Corrected 9B significantly beats continued 4B inference while processing zero historical prefix tokens. Evidence covers one direction, one geometry-matched Base-model pair, and 4K teacher-forced continuation; the near-native gate failed, the 16K branch was not run, and free-generation equivalence and a general state interface remain unproven.

</details>


### [43] [Mitigating LLM Over-Refusal via Dynamic Semantic Routing Calibratione](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25049)
*Wang, Zixuan,Zhang, Bingjie,Zhao, He,Guo, Dandan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文分析了LLM安全对齐导致的过度拒绝现象，发现注意力机制中的过敏安全头是根本原因。提出无需训练的推理框架SRC，通过动态抑制过敏头和双分支logits融合来缓解过度拒绝，同时尽可能保持安全性。实验表明方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM安全对齐导致过度拒绝，即对安全相关的良性指令错误拒绝。

Method: SRC是一个训练无关的推理时框架，包含两个主要组件：(1) Hypersensitive Safety Heads定位与动态抑制：首先识别出在Hard-Safe提示上误触发的稀疏注意力头，这些头表现出异常注意力纠缠，将无害目标实体与拒绝语义强制绑定，导致高熵路由冲突；然后在推理阶段动态抑制这些头的激活，以减轻路由冲突。(2) 双分支logits融合：在解码过程中，将原始LLM分支与经过SRC调节的分支的logits进行融合，作为安全正则化器，以平衡过度拒绝缓解与安全保持。整个流程无需额外训练，直接应用于预训练LLM的推理过程，通过干预注意力机制和输出分布来恢复可信推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "过度拒绝缓解", "value": "未提供具体数值", "setting": "广泛实验"}, {"metric": "内在安全性", "value": "尽可能保持", "setting": "广泛实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过注意力内部路由冲突的视角对过度拒绝进行机制分析，发现稀疏的Hypersensitive Safety Heads在Hard-Safe提示上误触发并导致注意力纠缠，将无害目标实体与拒绝语义强制绑定。；提出语义路由校准（SRC），一种轻量级、训练无关的推理框架，精确定位并动态抑制这些过敏安全头。；引入双分支logits融合，在解码时作为安全正则化器，恢复可信推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) aligned for safety often suffer from over-refusal, incorrectly rejecting benign yet safety-related instructions. Prior studies primarily attribute this to static representation overlap, largely overlooking the underlying dynamic mechanisms. In this paper, we present the mechanistic analysis of over-refusal through the lens of internal routing conflicts within transformer attention. We discover that a sparse subset of Hypersensitive Safety Heads misfires on Hard-Safe prompts, exhibiting abnormal attention entanglement that forcefully binds harmless target entities to refusal semantics. This triggers a severe, high-entropy routing conflict that deprives target entities of necessary attention. To counteract this, we propose Semantic Routing Calibration (SRC), a lightweight, training-free inference framework. SRC precisely localizes and dynamically suppresses these hypersensitive safety heads at the inference stage. Coupled with a dual-branch logits fusion that acts as a safety regularizer during subsequent decoding, SRC seamlessly restores trustworthy reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SRC alleviates over-refusal, with intrinsic safety performance preserved as much as feasible.

</details>


### [44] [Prompt Breadth and Rollout Refresh Interact in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25048)
*Hu, Lingxiang,Xia, Tianle,Xu, Ming,Sun, Yiding,Shang, Linfang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过 3x3 析因实验揭示在线策略蒸馏中提示广度与 rollout 刷新的交互效应：固定训练预算下，提示多样性对准确率的影响方向取决于响应是否随策略更新刷新，交互效应达 4.07 个百分点。研究还发现推理预算（输出 token 限制）会反转两种策略的相对优势。为 LLM 蒸馏训练中提示数量选择与 rollout 策略设计提供了重要实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中需要多少提示（prompt 数量/广度），以及这一需求如何依赖于生成训练响应的学生策略快照数量（rollout 刷新频率）。

Method: 论文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）流程。学生策略在训练过程中生成响应，教师模型提供监督信号。实验设计为 3x3 析因：三个提示广度水平（提示库大小）与三个 rollout 刷新水平（生成响应的策略快照数量）。训练总预算固定为 14,080 条轨迹和 110 次优化器更新。在每次优化更新时，学生策略从当前快照生成响应（或使用冻结的初始策略响应），教师对这些响应提供目标分布，学生通过最小化与教师分布的差异进行更新。评估在数学推理基准上进行，测量平均准确率、答案完成度和响应 token 消耗。关键发现是提示广度与刷新频率之间存在强交互：当响应来自冻结的初始策略时，增加提示多样性反而降低性能；而当响应随策略更新而刷新时，增加提示多样性提升性能。这揭示了 OPD 中数据多样性与策略新鲜度之间的耦合。此外，推理预算（如 32K 输出限制）会反转两种策略的相对优势，因为周期性刷新模型在短预算下完成度更高，而冻结响应模型在长预算下准确率更高但消耗更多 token。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "24.09%", "setting": "10 个策略快照，8 个提示，固定 14,080 条轨迹和 110 次优化器更新"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "24.51%", "setting": "10 个策略快照，14,080 个不同提示，固定 14,080 条轨迹和 110 次优化器更新"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "21.16% → 19.05%", "setting": "响应冻结于初始策略，增加提示广度"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "23.61% → 25.57%", "setting": "按更新刷新响应，增加提示广度"}, {"metric": "交互效应", "value": "4.07 个百分点（95% 问题配对区间 [2.00, 6.28]）", "setting": "提示广度与 rollout 刷新的交互"}, {"metric": "平均准确率（32K 输出限制下）", "value": "冻结响应模型超越周期性刷新模型", "setting": "两种教师模型匹配比较，输出上限 32K"}, {"metric": "相对响应 token 消耗", "value": "1.7-1.8 倍", "setting": "冻结响应模型 vs. 周期性刷新模型，32K 输出限制下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 联合研究提示广度与 rollout 刷新两个控制变量，揭示二者之间的交互效应。；在固定 14,080 条轨迹和 110 次优化器更新的条件下，设计 3x3 析因实验，量化不同提示库大小与策略快照数量的影响。；发现提示广度对准确率的影响方向随 rollout 刷新方式反转：响应冻结于初始策略时，增加广度导致准确率从 21.16% 降至 19.05%；而按更新刷新时，广度增加反而使准确率从 23.61% 升至 25.57%，交互效应达 4.07 个百分点（95% 问题配对区间 [2.00, 6.28]）。；在两种教师模型下发现第二个反转：周期性刷新的模型在短预算下准确率和答案完成度更高，但在 32K 输出限制下平均准确率被冻结响应模型超越，后者使用 1.7-1.8 倍的响应 token。

Abstract: How many prompts does on-policy distillation (OPD) need, and how does the answer depend on the student policies that generate its training responses? We study these two controls jointly: prompt breadth and rollout refresh. A 3x3 mathematical-reasoning experiment fixes 14,080 trajectories and 110 optimizer updates while varying the prompt bank and the number of response-generating policy snapshots. With ten snapshots, eight prompts reach 24.09% average accuracy, close to 24.51% for 14,080 distinct prompts. With responses frozen at the initial policy, however, increasing breadth lowers accuracy from 21.16% to 19.05%; under per-update refresh, it raises accuracy from 23.61% to 25.57%. The resulting interaction is 4.07 percentage points, with a 95% question-paired interval of [2.00, 6.28]. Matched comparisons under two teachers reveal a second reversal: the periodic models have higher short-budget accuracy and answer completion, but frozen-response models overtake in average accuracy at a 32K output limit, using 1.7-1.8x as many response tokens. These results show that prompt efficiency in OPD can depend on both refresh and inference budget.

</details>


### [45] [Flash-dLLM: IO-Aware KV Caching and Parallel Decoding for Fast, Memory-Efficient Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796)
*Nguyen-Tri, Quan,Ranjan, Mukul,Shen, Zhiqiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Flash-dLLM 是面向扩散大语言模型的免训练推理加速框架，将 GPU 内存 I/O 识别为主要瓶颈，提出 I/O 感知融合 KV 缓存内核与 dLLM 自草稿自验证的并行解码策略。实验显示其相较最强基线 Elastic-Cache 在 GSM8K 上实现 5.1×、在 HumanEval 上实现 11.0× 加速，并提升内存效率与长序列、大批量可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）因缺乏有效的 KV 缓存与可扩展的并行解码机制导致实际部署推理效率低；并且现有加速方法孤立研究 KV 缓存与并行解码，忽略了二者联合应用时的 GPU 内存 I/O 瓶颈。

Method: Flash-dLLM 是一个免训练的 dLLM 推理加速框架。端到端流程为：输入序列在扩散式非自回归生成环境下进入模型；首先通过 I/O 感知的融合 KV 缓存内核管理并复用 KV 缓存，该内核针对 GPU 内存 I/O 这一主导瓶颈进行优化，减少缓存读写过程中冗余的内存搬运，从而降低显存流量开销；在该优化缓存机制之上，采用 KV 缓存驱动的草稿-验证（draft-and-verify）并行解码策略，由同一个 dLLM 同时承担草稿器与验证器角色，生成多个候选 token 并并行验证，无需额外辅助模型。该统一设计在保持生成质量的同时加快解码，并改善了对更长序列和更大批大小的可扩展性。整个框架不涉及训练或微调，仅改进推理阶段的缓存内核与解码流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理加速", "value": "5.1×", "setting": "对比先前最强基线 Elastic-Cache，在 GSM8K 数学推理基准上"}, {"metric": "推理加速", "value": "11.0×", "setting": "对比先前最强基线 Elastic-Cache，在 HumanEval 代码生成基准上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别 GPU 内存 I/O 为启用 KV 缓存的 dLLM 推理的主要瓶颈；提出 I/O 感知的融合 KV 缓存内核，减少冗余内存搬运；提出 KV 缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码策略，dLLM 自身同时作为草稿器和验证器，无需辅助模型；训练无关的推理加速框架，统一优化 KV 缓存与并行解码以改善长序列和大批量下的可扩展性

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by enabling non-autoregressive text generation. However, their practical deployment remains limited by inefficient inference, largely due to the absence of effective Key-Value (KV) caching and scalable parallel decoding mechanisms. Existing acceleration methods typically study KV caching and parallel decoding in isolation, overlooking the I/O bottlenecks that arise when cache reuse and parallel token verification are jointly applied. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{Flash-dLLM}$, a training-free inference acceleration framework for fast and memory-efficient dLLMs. Flash-dLLM first identifies GPU memory I/O as a dominant bottleneck in KV-cache-enabled dLLM inference and addresses it with an I/O-aware fused KV-cache kernel that reduces redundant memory movement. Building on this optimized cache mechanism, Flash-dLLM further proposes an efficient KV-cache-driven draft-and-verify decoding strategy, where the dLLM itself serves as both drafter and verifier without requiring an auxiliary model. This unified design enables faster decoding while preserving generation quality and improving scalability to longer sequences and larger batch size. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flash-dLLM consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art dLLM acceleration methods in both inference speed and memory efficiency. In particular, it achieves $5.1\times$ and $11.0\times$ speedups over prior strongest baseline Elastic-Cache on GSM8K and HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


### [46] [Diffusion Drafts, AR Verifies: Accelerating Document OCR with Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26638)
*Kim, Dohyun,Han, Sungjun,Kim, Hyungguk,Kim, Yusik,Shin, Jamin,Seo, Paul Hongsuck,Ahn, Hongjoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GravityOCR提出参数共享的AR-block-diffusion自推测解码框架，用扩散路径并行起草、因果AR路径验证，无需独立草稿模型即可每轮提交多个OCR token；结合AR路径上的GRPO（序列/结构级OCR奖励）在OmniDocBench v1.6上把Overall从94.92提升到95.16且不损失起草效率，最终接近GLM-OCR的95.48；在SGLang部署中平均每前向提交9.7个token，decode加速3.94×、端到端整页处理加速1.32×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归OCR视觉语言模型每个输出token需要一次串行解码，推理速度受限；而扩散式并行生成虽然可以一步预测多个token，但各token在彼此未知的情况下被预测，直接提交会引入错误。

Method: GravityOCR采用参数共享的AR-block-diffusion架构，单一权重同时承担两个角色：一条扩散路径以block为单位并行起草多个输出token，一条因果自回归路径对草稿进行验证。数据流为：输入文档图像经视觉编码得到图像表征，扩散起草模块在单个前向中并行生成一个token block；随后因果AR验证路径在已知前序token的条件下对草稿逐位置校验，只提交通过验证的token，未通过的进入下一轮迭代，因此每轮可提交多个token且无需独立草稿网络。训练目标包含两部分：一是以图像为条件的扩散起草损失，二是因果AR验证损失，联合优化共享参数。验证所用的因果AR路径还使模型可以直接用GRPO进行强化学习，定义序列级与结构级OCR奖励，从而避免扩散轨迹上的似然估计不可解问题，同时更新共享起草器参数。推理时在SGLang中执行上述greedy/自推测循环，图像与草稿验证复用同一模型权重，减少解码步数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "OmniDocBench v1.6 Overall", "value": "94.92 -> 95.16（AR-path GRPO）", "setting": "OmniDocBench v1.6，AR-path GRPO训练后，扩散起草效率未下降"}, {"metric": "OmniDocBench v1.6 Overall", "value": "95.48（原始GLM-OCR基线，最终模型接近该值）", "setting": "OmniDocBench v1.6，与未加速的GLM-OCR对比"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "每forward pass平均提交输出token数", "value": "9.7", "setting": "SGLang serving部署"}, {"metric": "decode-only加速比", "value": "3.94×", "setting": "SGLang部署，region crops区域裁剪输入，相对AR解码"}, {"metric": "端到端页处理加速比", "value": "1.32×", "setting": "SGLang部署，整页文档处理，相对AR解码"}]}

Conclusion: GravityOCR：参数共享的AR-block-diffusion模型，同时训练并行扩散起草与因果AR验证，无需独立草稿网络；先验证后提交的自推测解码机制，每轮可提交多个输出token；利用因果AR路径实现GRPO，配合序列级与结构级OCR奖励，避开扩散轨迹的似然估计同时更新共享起草器参数；在SGLang中实现serving部署，获得实测端到端页处理加速

Abstract: Autoregressive OCR vision-language models accurately convert document images into text and structured markup, but require one sequential decoding step per output token, limiting inference speed. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce errors. We therefore introduce GravityOCR, a parameter-shared AR-block-diffusion model jointly trained for parallel drafting and causal AR verification. Verifying drafts before commitment lets the model commit multiple output tokens per round without a separate drafting network. The causal AR path also enables GRPO with sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, avoiding diffusion-trajectory likelihood estimation while updating the shared drafter parameters. On OmniDocBench v1.6, AR-path GRPO improves the Overall score from 94.92 to 95.16 without reducing diffusion drafting efficiency, while the final model remains close to the original GLM-OCR score of 95.48. In an SGLang serving deployment, GravityOCR commits an average of 9.7 output tokens per forward pass and achieves a $3.94\times$ decode-only speedup on region crops and a $1.32\times$ end-to-end page-processing speedup over AR decoding.

</details>


### [47] [Spoken Language Models that Think Aloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26488)
*Ao, Junyi,Peng, Kainan,Ma, Mingbo,Zhang, Shun,Tang, Zhenyu,Ma, Xutai,Li, Xiang,Li, Yinghao,Wang, Yuancheng,Wu, Zhizheng,Li, Haizhou,He, Qing,Liu, Xubo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对语音语言模型中串行思考导致的静默问题，提出异步think-aloud框架，通过并行推理流和轻量级进度话语流，动态平衡以保持实时交互，实验显示在保持答案准确性的同时显著减少静默时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语音语言模型中直接应用串行“先思考后说话”的链式推理会导致长时间静默，破坏实时语音交互体验。

Method: 论文提出基于Thinker-Talker架构的异步think-aloud框架。系统包含两个流：主推理流负责逻辑推理，生成最终的完整回答；轻量级think-aloud流根据用户输入和不断演化的推理状态生成简短的、任务相关的进度话语。动态平衡策略在运行时协调两个流：当主推理流处于长时间计算时，触发think-aloud流产生语音以避免静默；当最终响应准备就绪时，取消所有待处理的think-aloud话语。具体实现细节、训练目标和推理过程未在摘要中详述。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 异步think-aloud框架：在Thinker-Talker架构中并行维护主推理流和轻量级think-aloud流，后者生成短的任务相关的进度话语。；动态平衡策略：运行时协调两个流，触发额外think-aloud语音以避免静默间隔，并在最终响应就绪时取消待处理话语。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has improved the capability of language models, directly applying it to Spoken Language Models (SLMs) may introduce long silent intervals under the serial "think-then-speak" paradigm, disrupting real-time spoken interaction. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous think-aloud framework for reasoning-based SLMs within the Thinker-Talker architecture. The framework maintains a primary reasoning stream for logical deduction and a lightweight think-aloud stream that generates short, task-grounded progress utterances conditioned on the user input and the evolving reasoning state. A dynamic balance strategy coordinates the two streams at runtime, triggering additional think-aloud speech to avoid silent gaps and canceling pending utterances when the final response becomes ready. Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.

</details>


### [48] [Layout-Guided Masking for GROBID: Lightweight Structural Gains in Large-Scale Scientific PDF Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26381)
*Foppiano, Luca,Khamassi, Sana,Gupta, Vipul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出在GROBID上增加轻量级CPU区域检测与类型化掩码路由，用于图、表、副文本的定位与过滤。在两个PMC语料和Table-BRGM基准上，与plain GROBID及多个GPU视觉系统对比，在段落精度、章节检测、表格检测F1（0.16→0.94）和表格结构（0.27→0.78）上有提升，且CPU端到端成本显著低于GPU方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模科学PDF摄取场景中，如何在CPU上低成本、低噪声地提升结构抽取质量，同时避免GPU视觉解析器的高成本和文本噪声问题。

Method: 该方法在GROBID的模块化字体流解析框架上扩展。输入为科学PDF页面，首先由轻量级CPU检测器定位图、表及副文本（页眉、页脚、页码）区域，并将这些区域编码为带类型的区域掩码。随后，掩码token被路由到GROBID的专用模型（如表格结构模型或图注链接模型），或直接被丢弃，从而避免将噪声区域送入正文结构解析流程。GROBID本身基于字体流与版面特征进行模块化解析，输出机器可读的全文及章节、段落、图注等结构。训练目标与优化遵循GROBID现有模块及新增区域检测/路由模块的监督学习流程。推理时完全在CPU上运行，端到端低成本完成PDF到结构化全文的转换。该方法解决的问题是：在不引入GPU视觉解析器噪声与高成本的前提下，利用类型化区域掩码过滤和定向路由提升结构抽取质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "章节结构 NS", "value": "+0.025", "setting": "Bioinformatics 语料（1,926篇），对照 plain GROBID，JATS section-aware 协议"}, {"metric": "章节结构 NS", "value": "+0.013", "setting": "Materials Science 语料（2,595篇），对照 plain GROBID"}, {"metric": "段落 recall", "value": "+0.086", "setting": "Materials Science 语料，对照 plain GROBID，dz=1.08"}, {"metric": "表格检测 F1", "value": "0.16 → 0.94", "setting": "Table-BRGM 外部基准"}, {"metric": "表格结构 GriTS-Top", "value": "0.27 → 0.78", "setting": "Table-BRGM 外部基准，低于最强GPU系统"}, {"metric": "段落 precision", "value": "两个语料上最佳", "setting": "对比 Docling、MinerU、olmOCR、dots.ocr 四个视觉系统"}, {"metric": "章节检测", "value": "Materials Science 上最佳", "setting": "对比四个视觉系统"}, {"metric": "字符错误率", "value": "与最佳GPU解析器差距在0.004以内", "setting": "对比四个视觉系统"}, {"metric": "端到端CPU成本", "value": "比最便宜GPU系统Docling低2.7–3.2倍，比生成式解析器低10–14倍", "setting": "端到端CPU运行成本对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将轻量级CPU检测器定位的图、表、页眉、页脚、页码等区域编码为类型化区域掩码，并将掩码token路由到GROBID专用模型或丢弃。；保持GROBID的模块化字体流解析架构，仅在CPU上增加区域检测与掩码路由，实现低成本的端到端结构提升。；提出面向章节感知的结构化评测协议，在JATS基准上评估段落、章节、图注链接等结构指标。

Abstract: Transforming scholarly PDFs into machine-readable fulltext remains a bottleneck for large-scale information systems. Recent vision-based parsers improve accuracy, but need GPUs and may introduce noise into the extracted text. GROBID, a modular font-stream parser running on CPU, is the de-facto standard for structuring scientific articles and underpins several of the largest open scholarly corpora. We pair it with a lightweight CPU detector localising figure, table, and paratext (header, footer, page number) regions, encoded as typed-area masks whose tokens are routed to GROBID's specialised models or discarded. On two PMC corpora, Bioinformatics (1,926 articles) and Materials Science (2,595), scored against JATS with a section-aware structural protocol, our extension improves over plain GROBID on most metrics (NS $+0.025$/$+0.013$; $+0.086$ paragraph recall on Materials Science, $d_z{=}1.08$), and caption-linked figure recovery improves on both corpora. On the external Table-BRGM benchmark, table detection recovers F1 $0.16 \to 0.94$ and table structure follows (GriTS-Top $0.27 \to 0.78$, below the strongest GPU system). On body text, against four vision-based systems (Docling, MinerU, olmOCR, dots.ocr), it has the best paragraph precision on both corpora, the best section detection on Materials Science, and a character error rate within 0.004 of the best GPU parser. End-to-end on CPU, it costs $2.7$--$3.2\times$ less than the cheapest GPU system (Docling) and $10$--$14\times$ less than generative parsers.

</details>


### [49] [HySparse2: Hybrid Sparse Attention with Two-Level KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26368)
*Wei, Jianyu,Gao, Yizhao,Zhang, Qihao,Chen, Shimao,Tang, Zhengju,Cheng, Yu,Zhou, Shengjie,Jiang, Zihan,Song, Yifan,Zhang, Hailin,Zhao, Liang,Yang, Bo,Wang, Gang,Cao, Shijie,Luo, Fuli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HySparse2是一种两级KV共享的混合稀疏注意力架构，针对长视野多轮智能体场景，通过KV Bridging和token级KV Reuse降低预填充计算与KV缓存存储，并在80B-A3B MoE模型上优于HySparse和Hybrid SWA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野、多轮智能体场景下，预填充计算与KV缓存存储随上下文增长而急剧膨胀，同时需要保持长上下文检索准确性。

Method: HySparse2采用两级KV共享的混合稀疏注意力架构。外层为KV Bridging：模型包含自解码器与交叉解码器，但仅对全注意力层进行桥接。自解码器使用混合滑动窗口注意力（SWA），处理输入序列并生成隐藏状态。交叉解码器使用混合稀疏注意力，其全注意力层的KV缓存直接从自解码器对应全注意力层的隐藏状态生成，而非重新计算。内层保留HySparse的KV Reuse设计并做两点改进：一是将块级稀疏替换为token级稀疏，使长上下文检索更细粒度；二是移除稀疏层中独立的SWA分支，转而强制将最近token的滑动窗口纳入稀疏选择。该设计使得所有交叉解码器KV缓存均可由自解码器隐藏状态构建，因此预填充阶段在完成自解码器后即可退出，跳过所有交叉解码器层，从而显著降低预填充计算量和KV缓存存储。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "长上下文检索与多轮智能体任务性能", "value": "优于HySparse和Hybrid SWA", "setting": "80B-A3B MoE模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 外层KV Bridging：采用YOCO风格的自解码器-交叉解码器结构，但仅桥接全注意力层；自解码器使用混合滑动窗口注意力，交叉解码器使用混合稀疏注意力。；内层KV Reuse改进：用token级稀疏替代块级稀疏，实现更细粒度长上下文检索；移除稀疏层中独立的SWA分支，将最近token的滑动窗口强制纳入稀疏选择。；所有交叉解码器KV缓存均从自解码器隐藏状态构建，预填充可在自解码器后退出，跳过所有交叉解码器层，减少预填充计算与KV缓存存储。

Abstract: Long-horizon and multi-turn agents typically generate short actions and process long observations from tools and environments. This growing context demands efficient prefill, compact KV-cache storage, and accurate long-context retrieval. To meet these demands, we introduce HySparse2, a hybrid sparse attention architecture with two-level KV sharing. At the outer level, KV Bridging adopts a YOCO-style self-decoder and cross-decoder structure, but bridges only full-attention layers. The self-decoder uses hybrid sliding-window attention (SWA), while the cross-decoder uses hybrid sparse attention. The KV caches for full-attention layers in the cross-decoder are generated from the hidden states of full-attention layers in the self-decoder. At the inner level, HySparse2 retains HySparse's core KV Reuse design with two refinements. First, it replaces block-level sparsity with token-level sparsity for finer long-context retrieval. Second, it removes the separate SWA branch from sparse layers and instead forces a sliding window of recent tokens into the sparse selection. This two-level KV sharing allows all cross-decoder KV caches to be constructed from self-decoder hidden states. Prefill can therefore exit after the self-decoder, skipping all cross-decoder layers. On an 80B-A3B MoE model, HySparse2 outperforms HySparse and Hybrid SWA on long-context retrieval and multi-turn agentic tasks, while substantially reducing prefill computation and KV-cache storage.

</details>


### [50] [PACE-dLLM: Elastic Block Decoding via Confidence Cliff Estimation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26249)
*Lu, Xiaocheng,Guo, Shuhan,Ma, Ziyue,Zhang, Jie,Liu, Jian,Guo, Jingcai,Che, Haoxuan,Guo, Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PACE-dLLM提出一种基于置信度断崖估计的自适应块解码方法，通过每步拟合置信度断崖确定前瞻视野，并独立阈值提交token，在LLaDA和Dream上实现5.23x和3.06x的平均加速，同时保持最佳准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块式解码中，块长度B耦合了前瞻视野和可提交token数量两个决策，导致固定块长在下溢时增加每token必要前向次数（NFE），上溢时无有用收益。如何从模型置信度中自动确定最优前瞻视野并独立控制token提交？

Method: PACE-dLLM针对扩散语言模型（dLLM）的块式并行解码进行加速。标准块式解码中，每次前向预测长度为B的块，并提交高置信度token。但B耦合了前瞻视野和提交数量，导致效率低下。PACE-dLLM观察到窗口内置信度随位置变化呈现断崖式下降，其饱和点对应于合适的前瞻视野。具体流程：在每一步解码中，模型对当前窗口内各位置的token置信度进行估计，然后拟合一个参数化断崖函数（闭式解），该函数的饱和点决定了下一步的前瞻视野（即块长度）。提交token时使用独立的置信度阈值，仅提交高于阈值的token，与前瞻视野解耦。理论分析表明，在饱和产出抽象下，断崖锚定的视野能保证最大的有用每遍产出，且固定视野若小于该点会导致渐近NFE率更差，若大于该点则无额外有用产出。推理时，该过程每步自适应调整，实现更快的生成速度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "最佳", "setting": "四个推理与代码基准，两个开源dLLM基座（LLaDA和Dream）"}, {"metric": "平均墙钟时间加速", "value": "5.23x", "setting": "LLaDA基座，对比未加速半自回归基线"}, {"metric": "平均墙钟时间加速", "value": "3.06x", "setting": "Dream基座，对比未加速半自回归基线"}, {"metric": "最大墙钟时间加速", "value": "8.52x", "setting": "数学任务上，LLaDA基座"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现扩散语言模型每步窗口内置信度呈现上下文相关的断崖（confidence cliff），其饱和点直接指示适当的前瞻视野；提出PACE-dLLM，每步闭式拟合参数化断崖，以饱和点设定下一前瞻视野，并用独立置信度阈值决定token提交；在饱和产出抽象下证明断崖锚定视野是达到最大有用每遍产出的最小视野：固定视野下溢时渐近NFE率更差，上溢时无额外有用产出

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs), such as LLaDA and Dream, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) LLMs in generation quality while supporting native parallel decoding. A standard acceleration strategy is block-wise decoding, where each forward pass predicts a block of length B and commits high-confidence tokens. However, B couples two distinct decisions: the look-ahead horizon and the number of tokens to commit. Existing accelerators address this limitation through indirect heuristics, such as volatility tracking, delimiter detection, and learned scoring. In contrast, we show that the required information is already encoded in the model's own per-step confidence: in-window confidence typically follows a context-dependent cliff, whose saturation point directly identifies the appropriate look-ahead horizon. We propose PACE-dLLM, which fits this parametric cliff in closed form at each step, sets the next horizon by its saturation point, and uses an independent confidence threshold for token commitment. Under a saturated-yield abstraction, we show that the cliff-anchored horizon is the smallest horizon attaining maximal useful per-pass yield: fixed horizons that undershoot it incur a worse asymptotic NFE rate, while overshooting adds no useful yield. On four reasoning and code benchmarks, PACE-dLLM achieves the best average accuracy on both open-source dLLM backbones, with average wall-clock speedups of 5.23x on LLaDA and 3.06x on Dream (up to 8.52x on math) over the unaccelerated semi-AR baseline, advancing the quality-throughput Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [51] [Modality-Gated Deep Adapters: Adding a Modality to a Frozen Embedding Model with Exact Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26182)
*Tonmoy, Abdul Basit,Hoque, Kazi Fardinul,Arham, Md. Shahrier Islam,Luthra, Arman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出模态门控深度适配器，在冻结的2B多模态嵌入LLM上添加新模态而精确保持原有输出不变。音频包使音频-文本R@10提升3.4-5.4点，热成像包使热成像-文本R@10从0.224提升到0.785。方法在训练后仍保持不变性，推理时无需路由信息，并释放模型与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效适配方法（如LoRA）在向冻结的多模态嵌入模型添加新模态时会静默改变基座模型的文本路径输出，破坏已存储的嵌入和检索索引，无法保证已有输出的精确不变性。

Method: 基座是一个冻结的多模态嵌入LLM（2B参数），其自身包含文本路径和视觉路径。在每个解码器层附加瓶颈适配器，多个适配器按模态分组为“包”（如音频包、热成像包）。每个包仅在编码其对应模态时激活，执行适配器计算；对于未被任何包声明处理的输入，计算完全走基座模型原有计算图，输出逐比特不变。当多个包共同加载时，通过一个精确零隔离矩阵组合各包的输出，保证包间互不影响。音频模态以连接器令牌形式注入，热成像模态复用基座已有的冻结视觉路径。训练目标为适配新模态的嵌入对齐，但训练不会破坏基座原有输出的不变性。推理时无需任务标签或路由元数据，系统根据输入模态自动路由到对应包。该架构解决了在不改变已部署嵌入模型现有输出的前提下增量添加新模态的难题，同时实现新模态的有效编码。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "audio-to-text R@10", "value": "+3.4 至 +5.4 点", "setting": "在一个冻结的2B基座上，音频包（以连接器令牌注入）对比同样训练的控制组，所有随机种子均为正，并在11倍数据上复现"}, {"metric": "thermal-to-text R@10", "value": "0.224 到 0.785", "setting": "热成像包复用基座自有的冻结视觉路径，在所有种子上约七倍通过预先注册的接受门槛"}, {"metric": "CLAP-style 比较中的外部音频编码器差距", "value": "16 R@10 点", "setting": "在CLAP风格比较中排名高于Whisper系列的外部音频编码器，在冻结LLM内部反而落后16个R@10点"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 模态门控深度适配器：在每个解码器层附加瓶颈适配器，按模态分组为包，仅当编码对应模态时执行。；精确输出保持：未被任何包声明处理的输入经过基座模型自身计算图，输出逐比特不变。；精确零隔离矩阵：共同加载的多个模态包通过隔离矩阵组合，互不干扰。；训练后仍保持性质：不变性在任意训练后成立，非仅初始化时成立。；推理时无需任务标签或路由元数据。；释放音频模型、热成像包及训练、评测、不变性套件。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding models are deployed at scale: retrieval indices, benchmark results, and behavioral audits all depend on the base model's exact outputs. Extending such a model to a new modality with existing parameter-efficient methods silently changes those outputs; LoRA-style adaptation rewrites the text path whether or not the weights are merged, invalidating every stored embedding. We propose modality-gated deep adapters: bottleneck adapters attached to every decoder layer of a frozen multimodal embedding LLM, grouped into per-modality packs that execute only while their own modality is being encoded. The result is a modality added with zero change to existing outputs: inputs no pack claims traverse the base model's own computation graph, bit-for-bit unchanged, and co-loaded packs compose with an exact-zero isolation matrix. Both properties are stated as propositions, hold after arbitrary training rather than only at initialization, require no task labels or routing metadata at inference, and are verified by exact-equality tests on the released checkpoints. On one frozen 2B base, the audio pack (injected as connector tokens) improves audio-to-text R@10 by +3.4 to +5.4 points over an identically trained control, positive at every seed and reproduced at eleven times the data; the thermal pack, reusing the base's own frozen vision path, clears its pre-registered acceptance gate roughly sevenfold at every seed and lifts thermal-to-text R@10 from 0.224 to 0.785. An encoder swap locates the missing capacity: an external audio encoder that outranks Whisper-family encoders in CLAP-style comparisons loses by 16 R@10 points inside the frozen LLM, so the capacity belongs in the layers, exactly where the gated adapters place it. We release the audio model, the thermal pack, and the training, evaluation and invariance suites: models at huggingface.co/EximiusLabs, code on GitHub.

</details>


### [52] [Optimizing Denoising Trajectories in dLLMs: A Lightweight Evolutionary Heuristic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26052)
*Zhao, Zijian,Jin, Dian,Tong, Xialiang,Li, Sen,Yuan, Mingxuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对扩散大语言模型推理中的去噪调度问题，诊断了现有置信度启发式调度器失效的注意力机制根源，并提出一种仅393参数的轻量级演化启发式调度器。该方法利用CMA-ES动态整合多个启发式特征与上下文嵌入，在LLaDA和Dream模型上于四个推理规划基准上超越了常规启发式方法和SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）在推理时依赖精心设计的去噪调度器，但现有基于置信度的启发式调度器存在两大失效模式：EOS溢出（EOS Overflow）和近端偏置（Proximal Bias），导致生成质量下降。同时，单一启发式指标无法在所有场景下都表现最优，最优去噪轨迹高度依赖上下文。

Method: 未在摘要中提供完整架构细节。已知：调度器动态整合多个启发式特征与一个上下文平均场嵌入；使用协方差矩阵自适应演化策略（CMA-ES）进行优化，仅有393个可训练参数。其输入应包含当前去噪步骤的若干统计量（如模型输出的置信度、有效注意力分数等），输出为下一步去噪的决策（如选择哪个位置去噪或是否停止）。训练目标为通过演化策略最大化生成质量。具体模块顺序、数据流、训练流程及推理流程未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "生成质量（具体指标未在摘要中披露）", "value": "超越强基线，包括常规启发式方法、块自回归方法以及近期SOTA方法", "setting": "在LLaDA和Dream两个模型上，四个推理与规划基准测试中"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示了基于置信度的调度器失效原因：某些位置对无效token（如[MASK]和[EOS]）赋予过高注意力权重，产生误导性置信信号；发现有效注意力分数可以作为传统置信度启发式的互补指导；提出一种轻量级演化启发式调度器：使用CMA-ES优化，动态整合多个启发式特征与上下文平均场嵌入，仅393个可训练参数，是迄今参数最高效的神经调度器；通过演化策略自适应地学习上下文相关的去噪轨迹，克服了固定启发式的局限性

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to conventional Auto-Regressive (AR) Large Language Models (LLMs). By leveraging bidirectional attention and parallel decoding, dLLMs enable more efficient generation. However, they require a carefully designed denoising scheduler at inference time (absent during training) whose choice significantly impacts generation quality. While confidence-based heuristic schedulers have shown strong empirical performance, they suffer from two critical failure modes: EOS Overflow and Proximal Bias. Through in-depth analysis of the Transformer's attention patterns, we reveal that these failures stem from certain positions assigning disproportionately high attention weights to invalid tokens (e.g., [MASK] and [EOS]), which produce misleading confidence signals. Building on this insight, empirical evidence shows that valid attention scores can provide complementary guidance to conventional confidence-based heuristics, yet no single metric consistently excels across all scenarios, implying that the optimal denoising trajectory is highly context-dependent. To address this problem, we propose a lightweight evolutionary heuristic scheduler optimized using the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES). Our scheduler dynamically integrates multiple heuristic features with a contextual mean-field embedding, while requiring only 393 trainable parameters. Evaluated on LLaDA and Dream across four reasoning and planning benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms strong baselines, including conventional heuristics, block auto-regressive methods, and recent State-Of-The-Art (SOTA) approaches. To the best of our knowledge, it represents the most parameter-efficient neural scheduler to date. Our code is available at https://github.com/RS2002/Evo-Denoise .

</details>


### [53] [Truth for Believable AI: Expressed Doubt, Provenance, and Belief Revision as an Engineerable Stance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26035)
*Cochinescu, Sebastian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在固定语言模型上实现了一个工程化行为层，用于表达不确定性、追踪来源和修正信念，并构建了多会话基准进行机械评分。在Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct上，审计确认保证100%成立，真实修正比虚假修正更易被接受（持有信念0.44 vs 0.15），但预指定的表达保真度、矛盾分离和来源边际未达标。事后分析发现平均答案token概率门控AUC仅0.41，而采样一致性门控AUC达0.66，后者在独立协议下复现。结论限于构造性审计保证和该基准下的门控失败，需扩展事实基数后再进行人类评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在固定语言模型之上以可工程化的行为层实现表达不确定性、来源感知断言与显式信念修正，并为多会话代理提供可审计、可复现的行为评估。

Method: 系统是构建在固定语言模型之上的行为层，包含三个认知状态（具体未在摘要中列举）、每条主张的置信度与类型化来源、一个来源门控的表达规则，以及一个持久化修正存储，该存储提供可审计确认并对虚假纠正具有部分抵抗。推理时，模型生成回答，行为层根据每主张的置信度和来源决定是否表达不确定性或修正回答；修正存储跨会话持久化，并记录可审计确认。评估使用构造的、可机械评分的多会话基准，用合成模型验证五项检查，再用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct实测。事后分析比较了平均答案token概率门控与采样一致性门控，发现前者AUC 0.41低于随机，后者AUC 0.66，据此选择一致性门控并在独立协议下复现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "审计确认健全性", "value": "100%", "setting": "Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct，构造性保证，所有情况"}, {"metric": "真实修正接受率（持有信念）", "value": "0.44", "setting": "Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct，多会话基准"}, {"metric": "虚假修正接受率（持有信念）", "value": "0.15", "setting": "Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct，多会话基准"}, {"metric": "真实修正接受率（含规则接受未持有事实修正）", "value": "0.875", "setting": "Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct，多会话基准"}, {"metric": "虚假修正接受率（含规则接受未持有事实修正）", "value": "0.420", "setting": "Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct，多会话基准"}, {"metric": "表达保真度、矛盾分离、来源边际", "value": "失败", "setting": "预指定指标，Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "平均答案token概率门控AUC", "value": "0.41", "setting": "事后分析，端到端，按对话聚类"}, {"metric": "采样一致性门控AUC", "value": "0.66", "setting": "事后分析，端到端，按对话聚类"}, {"metric": "一致性门控配置满足操纵检验与能力等价标准", "value": "通过（选择依赖）", "setting": "独立承诺协议，重抽对话集复现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将表达不确定性、类型化来源断言与显式信念修正组合为固定LLM之上的行为层，包含三种认知状态与来源门控表达规则。；设计带审计确认与对虚假纠正部分抵抗的持久化修正存储。；构建可机械评分的多会话基准，包含五个构造性检查项，并在合成模型与Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct上评估。；发现基于平均答案token概率门控端到端AUC仅0.41（低于随机），而基于采样一致性的门控达到AUC 0.66，并据此在独立承诺协议下复现一致性门控配置。

Abstract: Conversational agents often express answers in a uniformly confident register. We test whether expressed uncertainty, provenance-aware assertion, and explicit belief revision can be implemented as a behavior layer over a fixed language model; we do not test believability or trust. The layer combines three epistemic states, per-claim confidence and typed provenance, a provenance-gated expression rule, and a persistent revision store with auditable acknowledgments and partial resistance to false corrections. We evaluate it on a constructed, mechanically scored multi-session benchmark using a synthetic model and Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. The synthetic instrument passes all five checks. On the real model, acknowledgment soundness, a by-construction guarantee, holds in 100% of cases, and true corrections are accepted more often than false ones (0.44 vs. 0.15 on held beliefs; 0.875 vs. 0.420 including rule-accepted corrections of unheld facts), but the pre-specified expression-fidelity, contradiction-separation, and provenance margins fail. A disclosed post hoc analysis shows that expression gated on mean answer-token probability ranks correctness below chance end to end (AUC 0.41, conversation-clustered), whereas gating on sampling consistency discriminates (AUC 0.66). A consistency-gated configuration selected from this finding and evaluated under a separately committed protocol meets the conversation-level manipulation and capability-equivalence criteria and replicates on a redrawn conversation set. The manipulation result is selection-dependent, and both criteria remain unresolved when uncertainty is clustered over the 60 facts. The supported conclusions are limited to the by-construction audit guarantee, store-dependent partial correction discrimination, and a benchmark- and model-specific failure of token-probability gating; scaling the fact base is required before human evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [MemoryAthena: Adaptive Routing over Latent and Generated Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25853)
*Li, Mingyuan,Yu, Guangsheng,Zhang, Juyuan,Wang, Xu,Man, Zhibo,Zhang, Haonan,Ji, Shaoxiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemoryAthena提出在冻结LLM上通过因果路由动态融合直接检索记忆与生成记忆，在问答和NLP任务上平均准确率分别提升1.63和2.40个点，展示了生成记忆作为选择性修正的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式记忆而非仅检索式记忆能否作为直接检索的有效补充？何时、选择哪个以及以多大强度让生成记忆介入，是核心挑战。

Method: MemoryAthena在冻结的因果骨干模型基础上，引入显式记忆表Engram及读取器。系统包含三种通路：1) 直接Engram检索(E)：从记忆表中检索并读取，作为锚点；2) 生成式Engram(GE)：以检索到的Engram线索为条件生成记忆表示；3) 生成式隐藏(GH)：不查阅记忆表，仅从骨干的因果状态生成记忆表示。三类通路输出在残差空间中融合：E的残差作为基础，GE和GH的生成表示通过一个轻量级因果路由头的门控机制被选择性地接纳；若接纳，则通过有界插值修改E的残差；若拒绝，则精确恢复E的原始残差。路由头在骨干、记忆、生成器和读取器全部冻结的情况下训练，训练目标基于反事实的未来token似然优势，即比较GE和GH相对于E的条件概率增益。整个记忆侧（不含冻结骨干）约201M参数，支持在推理时动态决定是否、选择哪个以及以多大强度让生成记忆干预。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "五任务问答平均准确率", "value": "从37.65提升至39.28", "setting": "与直接路径同一检查点对比"}, {"metric": "六任务通用NLP平均", "value": "从76.73提升至79.13", "setting": "与直接路径同一检查点对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出三种记忆通路：直接Engram检索(E)、基于检索Engram线索的生成(GE)和基于因果骨干状态的生成(GH)。；设计轻量级因果路由头，通过反事实未来token似然优势训练，在骨干、记忆、生成器和读取器冻结的情况下学习何时让生成记忆介入。；推理时允许被接纳的生成候选通过有界插值修改E残差，拒绝时精确恢复直接通路。

Abstract: Learned-memory methods store information in an explicit table and consume it through a separate reader, allowing addressing, storage, and reading to be modified independently. We study whether useful memory can also be generated rather than only retrieved. MemoryAthena uses three pathways: direct Engram retrieval (E), generation from retrieved Engram cues (GE), and generation from causal backbone states without consulting the memory table (GH). Generated memory is conditionally useful: it can complement E in one context but interfere with it in another. MemoryAthena therefore treats E as an anchor and learns when a generated representation should intervene. With the backbone, memory, generators, and readers frozen, a lightweight causal routing head is trained from counterfactual future-token likelihood advantages of GE and GH relative to E. At inference time, an admitted candidate modifies the E residual through bounded interpolation, while rejection recovers the direct pathway exactly. On question answering, MemoryAthena raises the five-task average from 37.65 to 39.28 over the direct pathway of the same checkpoint, while the six-task general-NLP average increases from 76.73 to 79.13. The complete memory-side system contains approximately 201M parameters, excluding the frozen backbone. Further analyses show complementary strengths among E, GE, and GH across tasks and inputs. These results support generated memory as a selective correction to direct retrieval and highlight routing when, which, and how strongly to intervene as the central challenge.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态模型主要强调感知与交互，在多模态理解与推理、长时程智能体任务上能力不足，且缺乏原生支持音频与视频的智能体执行框架，实时多模态交互在上下文与记忆管理、工具使用与子智能体调度方面存在系统级挑战。

Conclusion: 原生多模态协同训练策略，在保持强文本能力的同时将智能体能力从文本迁移到音频与视频任务；继承Qwen3.8-Next的稀疏MoE架构并将上下文窗口扩展至一百万个token，支持长上下文多模态推理与长时程规划；发布Qwen-MM-Plugins轻量开源插件框架，为多模态生产力补齐智能体执行环境对原生音视频支持的缺失；将实时多模态交互定义为系统级挑战，发布Qwen-Live-Harness开源框架，编排上下文与记忆管理、工具使用与子智能体委派

Abstract: We introduce Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash, a natively multimodal agentic model for real-world multimodal productivity. Compared with previous omni models, which primarily emphasized perception and interaction, Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash substantially improves multimodal understanding and reasoning, as well as performance on long-horizon agentic tasks. These capabilities are supported by a native multimodal co-training strategy that preserves strong text-domain capabilities while facilitating the transfer of agentic capabilities from text to audio and video tasks. The model inherits the sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture of Qwen3.8-Next and extends the context window to one million tokens, supporting long-context multimodal reasoning and long-horizon planning. These advances enable integration into production workflows as a primary agent or a specialized sub-agent, supporting video editing, long-form audio and video translation, music-conditioned music video or movie generation, and video-based note or omni-skill creation. To address the lack of native audio and video support in existing agent harnesses, we release Qwen-MM-Plugins, a lightweight open-source plugin framework for multimodal productivity. We further frame real-time multimodal interaction as a system-level challenge requiring orchestration of context and memory management, tool use, and sub-agent delegation. Accordingly, we release Qwen-Live-Harness, an open-source framework for building responsive, real-time multimodal agents based on Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash achieves strong performance across multimodal understanding, reasoning, long-horizon agentic execution, and video productivity tasks. These results and the accompanying open-source tools support Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash as a practical foundation for deploying natively multimodal agents in research and production.

</details>


### [56] [Matryoshka attribution: Learning to attribute language model outputs to representations and weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25518)
*Arora, Aryaman,Acharya, Kirill,Hu, Nathan,Zhang, Yanzhe,Goodman, Noah,Jurafsky, Dan,Potts, Christopher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Matryoshka Attribution (MAttr)，通过可微分 sigmoid top-k 掩码和随机化稀疏度监督，学习将 LLM 输出归因到内部表征与权重。在 Mechanistic Interpretability Benchmark 官方排行榜排名第 1，识别稀疏可迁移电路；并展示通过 RL 训练识别微调导致的拒绝行为权重，恢复 1% 权重即可移除拒绝并保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将语言模型输出归因到其内部计算（表征与权重），现有基于因果干预、梯度或可学习掩码的方法要么计算不可行，要么难以识别真正因果重要的内部组件。

Method: MAttr 将归因建模为识别嵌套的内部组件子集，以最小化下游损失。用简单的可微分 sigmoid top-k 算子参数化掩码，掩码作用于模型的内部组件（如注意力头、MLP 神经元或权重子集）。训练时随机化 k 值，对所有稀疏度同时监督，从而学习到一个按归因分数排序的组件序列。训练目标是最小化下游任务损失。推理时按学到的排序选择 top-k 组件，形成稀疏且任务可迁移的电路。作为实际应用，将 MAttr 与强化学习结合，用于识别 LLM 微调中导致下游行为的权重变化。具体地，在拒绝判定分数上训练 MAttr，发现将 Llama 3.1 8B Instruct 的 1% 权重恢复到基模型状态即可移除拒绝行为且保持能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "official leaderboard rank", "value": "1", "setting": "Mechanistic Interpretability Benchmark (Mueller et al., 2025)"}, {"metric": "refusal removal", "value": "恢复 1% 的 Llama 3.1 8B Instruct 权重到基模型状态", "setting": "基于拒绝判定分数的训练目标，报告在移除拒绝的同时保持能力"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将归因形式化为寻找最小化下游损失的嵌套内部组件子集的问题；提出 Matryoshka Attribution (MAttr)，使用可微分 sigmoid top-k 算子参数化掩码；训练时随机化 k 对全稀疏度同时监督，学习组件按归因分数的排序；在 Mechanistic Interpretability Benchmark 官方排行榜排名第 1，识别稀疏且任务可迁移的电路；将 MAttr 与强化学习结合，识别 LLM 微调中导致下游行为的权重变化，应用于拒绝行为归因与权重恢复编辑

Abstract: Attributing language model outputs to their internal computations is an open problem in interpretability. Existing methods, which use causal interventions, gradients, or learnable masks, either are infeasibly expensive or struggle to identify actual causally-important internal computations. We propose framing attribution as the problem of identifying nested subsets of internal components which minimise a downstream loss. To learn this task, we introduce Matryoshka Attribution (MAttr), a mask learning method that parametrises the mask with a simple differentiable sigmoid top-$k$ operator. We supervise training over all sparsities simultaneously by randomising $k$ over training, resulting in a learned ordering of components by attribution score. MAttr achieves number 1 on the official leaderboard of the Mechanistic Interpretability Benchmark (Mueller et al., 2025); our method identifies sparse and task-transferrable circuits across varying circuit bases. As a practical application, we show that MAttr can be trained with reinforcement learning to identify weight changes responsible for downstream behaviours in LLM finetuning. We train MAttr on refusal judge scores and find that restoring $1\%$ of Llama 3.1 8B Instruct's weights to their base model state is sufficient to remove refusals while maintaining capabilities. We view MAttr as a successful formulation of interpretability into a learnable objective that we can tackle with gradient descent, and encourage future work along these lines.

</details>


### [57] [ufakzeka-1: Building and Evaluating a 151M-Parameter Turkish Language Model from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25081)
*Teke, Sait Furkan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ufakzeka-1是一个151M参数土耳其语解码器模型，从零预训练于13.5B tokens，总成本约286美元，并附带完整的评估套件。论文的核心贡献是构建与测量记录，分享了安全门控污染、训练种子方差和数据修复边界等可迁移发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何以极低成本从零构建并严格评估一个151M参数土耳其语解码器语言模型，同时识别小型模型训练中可迁移的工程陷阱与评估可靠性问题。

Method: 模型为151M参数（含嵌入182M）的仅解码器Transformer，从零预训练。输入为土耳其语文本，由专门训练的字节级分词器编码，达到1.77 tokens/word。预训练采用三阶段调度，具体阶段细节未在摘要中披露；后训练使用开放许可数据与生成数据混合进行指令微调以支持对话。评估流程包含发布门控、规则检查的5,508段对话扫描、评判对话和人工测试，所有提示均从训练数据中留出，并通过数据构建中的去污染和每次构建前运行的检查不变量脚本来保证。论文还报告了安全门控因用其自身问题编写训练数据而产生虚高读数，以及训练种子方差与配方差异相当等发现，但未提供模型内部模块交互或训练目标的更详细描述。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "分词效率", "value": "1.77 tokens/word", "setting": "土耳其语字节级分词器"}, {"metric": "安全门控通过数", "value": "64/64 (污染后) vs 34/64 (诚实评估)", "setting": "安全门控评估，规则检查"}, {"metric": "规则检查对话扫描", "value": "5,508 段对话", "setting": "评估套件"}, {"metric": "预训练数据量", "value": "13.5B tokens", "setting": "开放许可文本"}, {"metric": "参数规模", "value": "151M (含嵌入182M)", "setting": "解码器语言模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 土耳其语字节级分词器，达到1.77 tokens/word；三阶段预训练调度；开放许可与生成数据混合的后训练数据配方；发布门控、5,508轮对话规则检查扫描、评判对话与人工测试组成的评估套件，配合数据去污染与构建前不变量脚本；报告安全门控被自身问题生成数据污染后读数从34/64虚高至64/64；发现训练种子方差与不同配方间差异相当，单种子比较在小规模下无信息量；数据轮次仅修复数据中缺失的内容，而长上下文身份跟踪与多轮算术不随数据变化改善，归因于模型规模限制

Abstract: We describe ufakzeka-1, a 151M-parameter (182M with embeddings) decoder-only Turkish language model pretrained from scratch on 13.5B tokens of openly licensed text and instruction-tuned for chat, at a total cost of about \$286 in cloud GPU, API and notebook time. The contribution is not the model's capability, which is what a model this size can be expected to have, but the record of building and measuring it: a Turkish byte-level tokenizer at 1.77 tokens per word, a three-stage pretraining schedule, a post-training mixture of openly licensed and generated data, and an evaluation battery of release gates, a rule-checked sweep of 5,508 conversations, judged conversations and hand tests, all with prompts held out from the training data, enforced by decontamination inside the data build and by a checked-in invariant script we run before each build. We report three findings that we believe transfer to other small-model efforts: a safety gate that had been "fixed" with training data written from its own questions read 64/64 while the honest figure was 34/64; training-seed variance was as large as the spread across every recipe we tried, so single-seed comparisons at this scale are uninformative; and data rounds repaired only what was absent from the data, while identity tracking over long context and multi-turn arithmetic did not move across any data change we tried, which we read as limits of the model size rather than gaps in the data, a reading the next, larger model will test. Weights, the data recipe, the evaluation code and the spend ledger are released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [58] [ChainDoRA: Tensor-Train Factorized Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25058)
*Yeafi, Ashfak,Hasan, Mehedi,Islam, Md Khairul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChainDoRA将DoRA的方向分支由稠密低秩分解替换为连通的Tensor-Train链参数化，适配器秩作为TT边界秩、独立TT秩控制容量与参数成本。在LLaMA-7B、15,119条response-only适配样本、七项常识推理基准上，TT rank 16取得72.30%平均准确率，高于LoRA的69.88%和DoRA的69.39%，而可训练参数仅5.35M（DoRA为56.98M，减少90.62%）。未披露公司、生产部署或线上A/B结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DoRA在方向分支上仍保留稠密的LoRA式低秩分解，导致可训练参数量较大；如何在保持权重“幅值-方向”分解优势的前提下大幅降低方向适配器的参数成本并维持下游推理能力。

Method: 方法建立在DoRA的权重分解之上：将预训练权重分解为幅值向量与方向矩阵，方向矩阵再做低秩更新。ChainDoRA的改动在方向分支的参数化方式：不再用两个稠密低秩矩阵乘积，而是用连通的张量列（Tensor-Train）链来生成方向低秩因子。具体地，输入侧张量收缩与输出侧张量收缩通过一条TT链相连，适配器设定的秩作为输入侧与输出侧收缩之间的边界秩（决定适配器维度/接口），而一个独立的TT秩控制链内部各核张量的中间维度，从而解耦‘适配器接口秩’与‘表征容量’，精确调节参数开销。训练时仅更新幅值向量与该TT参数化的方向分支，冻结预训练权重，采用响应式（response-only）监督在15,119条样本上微调LLaMA-7B。推理时把TT链收缩得到的方向低秩更新与幅值缩放合并回权重，按DoRA式的前向计算进行，不额外引入在线推理模块（论文未披露推理延迟、吞吐或显存开销）。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "七项常识推理基准平均准确率", "value": "72.30%", "setting": "ChainDoRA，TT rank 16，LLaMA-7B，15,119条response-only适配样本，可训练参数5.35M"}, {"metric": "七项常识推理基准平均准确率（LoRA基线）", "value": "69.88%", "setting": "匹配的LoRA基线，LLaMA-7B，15,119条response-only样本，可训练参数56.10M"}, {"metric": "七项常识推理基准平均准确率（DoRA基线）", "value": "69.39%", "setting": "匹配的DoRA基线，LLaMA-7B，15,119条response-only样本，可训练参数56.98M"}, {"metric": "可训练参数减少相对DoRA", "value": "90.62%", "setting": "ChainDoRA 5.35M对比DoRA 56.98M，同设定"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用连通的Tensor-Train（TT）链构造DoRA方向分支的低秩因子，适配器秩作为输入侧与输出侧TT收缩的边界秩，另设独立TT秩控制表征容量与参数开销；在幅值-方向分解框架内实现可调的参数量-精度权衡，并给出TT秩与适配器放置位置的消融；在响应式（response-only）监督的受控设置下与参数量对齐的LoRA、DoRA基线比较，展示显著参数削减下的精度保持/提升

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks while updating only a small fraction of their pretrained parameters. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) uses two trainable low-rank matrices, while Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) further separates weight magnitude and direction but retains the dense LoRA-style factorization in its directional branch. We propose ChainDoRA, a weight-decomposed adaptation framework that constructs the directional low-rank factors from a connected Tensor-Train (TT) chain, where the adapter rank forms the boundary rank between input- and output-side TT contractions and an independent TT rank controls representation capacity and parameter cost. Under a controlled 15,119-example response-only adaptation setting with LLaMA-7B, ChainDoRA is evaluated against matched LoRA and DoRA baselines on seven commonsense reasoning benchmarks. ChainDoRA with TT rank 16 achieves a seven-task average accuracy of 72.30%, compared with 69.88% for LoRA and 69.39% for DoRA, while requiring only 5.35M trainable parameters versus 56.10M for LoRA and 56.98M for DoRA, corresponding to a 90.62% reduction relative to DoRA. Ablations over TT rank and adapter placement show controllable parameter-accuracy trade-offs, indicating that connected TT parameterization can substantially reduce the parameter cost of magnitude-direction adaptation while preserving, and in this setting improving, downstream reasoning performance.

</details>


### [59] [Peerify: Benchmarking Peer-Review Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25046)
*Daghighfarsoodeh, Alireza,Ebrahimi, Sajad,Ghorbanpour, Ali,Sadeghian, Soroush,Cheraghi, Radin,Arabzadeh, Negar,Bagheri, Ebrahim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Peerify 是一个面向同行评审的稿件依据声明验证流水线：将评审意见分解为原子声明、检索稿件证据并判定声明是否被支持，同时发布含 800 条真实 NeurIPS/ICLR 2024 评审声明的基准（300 条人工标注审计）。结果显示检索中心验证与声明分解至关重要，自动标签与人工共识一致率 90.3%（κ=0.87），而现成蕴含模型 macro-F1 低于 0.24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审中审稿人声明是否被稿件证据支持，长期依赖人工核查，耗时且难以规模化；需要自动化、以稿件为依据的声明级验证。

Method: Peerify 接受稿件与一条评审意见作为输入。第一阶段做声明分解：把评审意见拆成可独立验证的原子声明，以获得更细粒度的验证单元。第二阶段做证据检索：以每条原子声明为查询，从稿件中检索相关证据片段（论文对多种检索策略进行了对比评测）。第三阶段做判定：结合检索到的稿件证据，由语言模型判断该声明是否被稿件支持；论文同时纳入现成蕴含模型作为基线，用于对比生成式/判别式判定路径。训练与监督方面，论文并未训练新模型，而是使用采集自 NeurIPS 2024 和 ICLR 2024 真实评审交互的 800 条声明构成基准（其中 300 条人工标注用于审计自动构建的监督标签），在流水线内评测 SOTA 语言模型与检索策略。评测路线为离线基准评测，论文未披露任何在线部署、生产流量或服务化推理资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "自动标签与人工共识一致率", "value": "90.3%", "setting": "300 条人工标注审计子集"}, {"metric": "Cohen's kappa", "value": "0.87", "setting": "300 条人工标注审计子集"}, {"metric": "macro-F1", "value": "<0.24", "setting": "现成蕴含模型（off-the-shelf entailment models）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Peerify 流水线：将评审意见分解为原子声明、检索相关稿件证据、并判定每条声明是否被稿件支持；构建基于 NeurIPS 2024 与 ICLR 2024 真实评审交互的 800 条声明基准，含 300 条人工标注子集用于审计自动监督信号；系统评测不同检索策略与 SOTA 语言模型在流水线中的表现，并与蕴含基线对比，量化检索中心验证与声明分解的作用

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in scholarly publishing, yet verifying whether reviewer claims are supported by manuscript evidence remains a largely manual and time-consuming process. We present Peerify, a pipeline for manuscript-grounded verification of peer-review claims. Given a manuscript and a review comment, the Peerify pipeline decomposes reviews into atomic claims, retrieves relevant manuscript evidence, and determines whether each claim is supported by the paper. To support the development and evaluation of the pipeline, we construct a benchmark of 800 claims derived from authentic peer-review interactions collected from NeurIPS 2024 and ICLR 2024, including a 300-claim hand-labeled subset used to audit the automated supervision. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models and retrieval strategies within the Peerify pipeline, together with entailment baselines. Our results demonstrate the importance of retrieval-centered verification and claim decomposition, while highlighting the challenges posed by ambiguous and interpretive reviewer claims. Automated labels agree with human consensus on 90.3% of audited claims ($\kappa = 0.87$), while off-the-shelf entailment models stay below 0.24 macro-F1.

</details>
