### [60] [The Sirens' Song: When Proximal Background Context Overshadows Distant Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26718)
*Yang, Xiaoyu,Lu, Jie,Duan, Wei,Yu, En*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文识别了长上下文LLM中的 'Proximity Trap' 现象，指出远距离证据利用不足源于近端无关背景的累积竞争，并提出了 t 分布方向匹配机制 LYRA 来重塑注意力分布，同时引入了细粒度基准 ProxBench。在 LongBench-v2、RULER 和 LongBench 上取得了跨长度和任务的持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM中，由于大量任务无关的近端背景干扰，导致对远距离证据注意力不足（Proximity Trap）。

Method: LYRA 是一种 t 分布方向匹配机制，作用于长上下文 LLM 的注意力计算过程。其核心思想是重塑上下文检索分布：通过 t 分布（重尾分布）对注意力权重进行方向匹配，使注意力质量更多地分配给任务相关的远距离证据，同时保留相对位置信息。具体实现细节未在摘要中披露。训练目标或优化方式未详细说明。推理时，该机制直接应用于注意力模块，无需额外训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能提升", "value": "持续改进", "setting": "在 LongBench-v2、RULER 和 LongBench 上，跨不同上下文长度和任务类别"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别并定义了 Proximity Trap 问题：远距离证据利用不足主要源自与大量近端无关背景的累积竞争，而非距离本身。；提出 LYRA（Long-context heavY-tailed Relevance Alignment）：一种 t 分布方向匹配机制，重塑上下文检索分布，将更多注意力质量导向任务相关证据，同时保留相对位置信息。；提出 ProxBench：一个多级细粒度基准，用于评估在递增的近端背景干扰下远距离证据的利用能力。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs focus on retrieving distant evidence from extensive context, yet existing work has largely focused on overcoming distance alone. In this work, we identify the Proximity Trap, insufficient attention to distant evidence often arises less from distance itself than from cumulative competition with abundant, task-irrelevant proximal background. To address the Proximity Trap, we introduce LYRA (Long-context heavY-tailed Relevance Alignment), a t-distributed directional matching mechanism that reshapes the context retrieval distribution, directing more attention mass toward task-relevant evidence, while preserving the relative positional information encoded. Extensive experiments on LongBench-v2, RULER, and LongBench demonstrate consistent improvements across context lengths and task categories. We further introduce ProxBench, a multi-level fine-grained benchmark for evaluating distant evidence utilization under increasing proximal background interference. Project page: https://xiaoyuyoung.github.io/LYRA/

</details>


### [61] [Train Where the Quantized Model Goes: On-Policy Distillation for Low-Bit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26708)
*Chen, Yuanteng,Liu, Zhilei,Wang, Peisong,Shao, Yuantian,Li, Chuangyi,Wang, Weining,Qiu, Shuang,Li, Gang,Liu, Jing,Cheng, Jian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对亚3比特量化模型长链推理退化问题，指出QAD固定语料前缀训练与部署自回归轨迹之间的exposure bias被量化放大，提出在QAD检查点上追加在线策略蒸馏OPD，让学生经量化前向路径自行生成并由全精度教师在其自身前缀上监督，结合任务验证器奖励；在四个模型、2.79与1.88有效比特下，MATH-500保留率从35%提升至70%，HumanEval从66%提升至91%，推理增益超过匹配预算的继续teacher-forced QAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚3比特量化模型在长链数学与代码推理上严重退化，长生成易陷入重复循环并耗尽解码预算；作者将其归因于量化放大的exposure bias——QAD在固定语料前缀上teacher-forcing训练，而部署时量化偏差沿模型自身自回归轨迹累积。

Method: 框架为两阶段量化后训练流程。第一阶段QAD(量化感知蒸馏)：在固定语料前缀上进行teacher-forcing蒸馏，使学生适配量化前向路径，获得稳定的低比特初始化，可较好恢复短问答能力但长链推理受损。第二阶段OPD(在线策略蒸馏)：加载QAD检查点作为学生，学生通过部署时实际使用的量化前向路径自回归生成序列，冻结的全精度教师对学生自己生成的prefix提供稠密token级监督，同时叠加任务验证器奖励以引导正确推理路径；训练目标为token级蒸馏损失与验证器奖励的组合，从而把教师监督放在量化模型真实到达的轨迹上，缓解量化放大的exposure bias。部署时仅使用量化学生模型的前向路径推理，教师与验证器不参与，实现sub-3-bit长链推理能力恢复。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "BF16性能保留率(平均)", "value": "35% → 70%", "setting": "MATH-500，四个模型，2.79与1.88有效比特"}, {"metric": "BF16性能保留率(平均)", "value": "66% → 91%", "setting": "HumanEval，四个模型，2.79与1.88有效比特"}, {"metric": "推理增益对比", "value": "显著超过匹配预算下继续teacher-forced QAD", "setting": "匹配预算对比实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 显式提出并归因'量化放大的exposure bias'，指出QAD依赖固定语料前缀与部署自回归轨迹间的分布错配；提出OPD阶段：从QAD检查点出发，学生通过部署所用的量化前向路径自行生成，在全精度冻结教师对其自身前缀上获得监督；将稠密token级蒸馏信号与任务验证器奖励相结合；形成QAD(稳定低比特初始化)+OPD(在线策略推理恢复)的两阶段sub-3-bit框架

Abstract: Quantization-aware distillation (QAD) restores much of the short-form question-answering performance lost to sub-3-bit quantization, yet leaves mathematical and code reasoning substantially impaired. Long generations often degenerate into repetitive loops, exhausting the decoding budget without completing a solution. We trace this gap to quantization-amplified exposure bias: QAD trains on fixed corpus prefixes, while quantization-induced deviations compound along the model's own autoregressive trajectories. To address this mismatch, we introduce an on-policy distillation (OPD) stage that places teacher supervision where the quantized model actually goes. Starting from a QAD checkpoint, the student generates through the quantized forward path used at deployment and receives feedback from a frozen full-precision teacher on its own prefixes, combining dense token-level guidance with task-verifier rewards. Across four models at 2.79 and 1.88 effective bits, OPD raises average BF16 performance retention from 35% to 70% on MATH-500 and from 66% to 91% on HumanEval while preserving short-form performance, with reasoning gains substantially exceeding those of continued teacher-forced QAD in matched-budget comparisons. By coupling QAD's stable low-bit initialization with OPD's on-policy reasoning recovery, our framework provides a comprehensive sub-3-bit solution that preserves broad capabilities while restoring long-form reasoning.

</details>


### [62] [Greedy Decoding Is Not Precision-Invariant: Cross-Precision Output Divergence in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26621)
*Du, Gaoyuan,Khan, Anam Nawaz,Zhou, Rex,Liu, Xiaoyang,Chakrabarti, Deepayan,Suya, Fnu,Li, Xueping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示LLM贪心解码并非精度不变：BF16与FP16在相同硬件上产生不同输出，49-100%提示分歧。通过误差传播分析定位LM head top-2 margin为关键因素，提出选择性FP32 LM head重算，在低batch单流推理下以<4%延迟开销提升22-36pp一致率，并明确其适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贪心解码在BF16与FP16等不同精度下产生不同输出，破坏了确定性假设；需要理解误差传播机制并寻找低开销缓解方法。

Method: 论文围绕LLM自回归贪心解码的跨精度确定性展开。输入为相同模型权重、提示和贪心解码算法，但推理精度在BF16与FP16之间切换。分析将误差来源分为body误差与LM head误差：22层Transformer body累积的误差不能区分翻转与非翻转步骤，而最终LM head处top-2 logit margin相对于top-2候选方向扰动的大小决定是否发生token翻转。基于该机制，提出选择性FP32 LM head重算：在每次解码步监测top-2 logit margin，当margin低于阈值时对该步的LM head计算使用FP32，否则沿用低精度，从而以较低开销提升精确一致率。实验在A10G、L4、A100上评估六模型四batch size，验证五个预测。推理路径为：标准低精度前向传播 -> LM head margin检测 -> 条件触发FP32 LM head重算 -> 输出token。该方法为部分缓解而非普适确定性保证，当body主导误差、batch size>=8或端到端FP8时收益消失。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 实证发现贪心解码的跨精度输出分歧，49-100%提示出现分歧且单token翻转会级联为轨迹级分歧；提出误差传播分析，指出22层累积body误差不能区分翻转与非翻转步骤，结果主要取决于LM head处top-2 logit margin与top-2候选方向扰动的关系；提出五个关于干预结果的可测试预测，包括更广泛FP32计算反而降低一致性，实验全部验证；提出低开销干预：当margin低于阈值时选择性重算FP32 LM head，在低batch单流推理下以<4%延迟开销获得+22-36pp精确一致率；映射六模型四batch size的适用边界，并假设训练时精度稳定性是关键因素

Abstract: Greedy decoding from large language models is commonly treated as deterministic. We show it is not precision-invariant: the same model, prompt, and decoding algorithm produce different outputs in BF16 versus FP16 on identical hardware. Across our evaluations of six models (1.1B-7B parameters, four families; divergence additionally characterised at 12B) and three benchmarks, 49-100\% of prompts diverge; a single token flip often cascades into trajectory-level divergence. We develop an empirical error-propagation analysis and find that 22 layers of accumulated body error do not distinguish flipping from non-flipping steps; the outcome depends primarily on the top-two logit margin at the LM head relative to the directional perturbation between the top-two candidates. The analysis makes five testable predictions about intervention outcomes, including that applying more FP32 compute (broader scope) makes agreement worse. The experiments match all five predictions. The best-performing low-overhead intervention we evaluate, selective FP32 LM head recomputation, triggered only when the margin falls below a threshold, delivers +22-36 pp exact agreement on A10G (+12-21 pp on L4 and A100) at less than 4\% latency overhead in low-batch (batch size <=4) single-stream inference. We map the applicability boundary across six models and four batch sizes, and hypothesise that training-time precision stability is a determining factor. The method is a partial mitigation rather than a universal determinism guarantee: its benefit vanishes when body-originated error dominates, including at batch size >=8 and under end-to-end FP8 in our tests.

</details>


### [63] [PACT: From Credit Assignment to Critic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26355)
*Fu, Jiayan,Xu, Hang,Zhang, Yong,Luo, Zhaokai,Hu, Yao,Zhao, Dongyan,Chuan, Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACT通过理论定义token级信用分配，并提出演员优先的评论家训练方法，在数学推理和代码生成任务上超越PPO、GRPO等基线，为LLM后训练提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: token级信用分配缺乏公认的数学定义，以及演员-评论家训练中评论家与更新后策略的不对齐问题。

Method: PACT方法基于演员-评论家框架，首先根据三个正则条件定义token级信用。训练时，先使用当前演员策略与环境交互收集轨迹，并计算优势估计。然后按照Actor-then-Critic顺序，先更新演员策略参数，再利用更新后的演员策略对原有轨迹进行重要性采样校正，并用校正后的样本训练评论家网络，使其更准确地评估新策略下的状态价值。评论家训练目标是最小化价值预测误差，并通过重要性权重修正分布偏移。迭代执行此过程，直至策略收敛。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "72.87%", "setting": "智能体数学推理四个基准测试"}, {"metric": "平均准确率提升（较GRPO）", "value": "8.80个百分点", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "平均准确率提升（较PPO）", "value": "13.16个百分点", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "通过率", "value": "67.4%", "setting": "SWE-bench Verified"}, {"metric": "通过率提升（较PPO）", "value": "2.4个百分点", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "通过率提升（较GRPO）", "value": "2.0个百分点", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "通过率提升（较SAO）", "value": "3.8个百分点", "setting": "同上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出完整性、前缀一致性和中性三个正则条件，唯一确定token级信用分配；理论解释on-policy蒸馏中教师模型作为隐式评论家，以及RLOO与token级信用的关系；设计PACT训练流程：先更新演员再更新评论家，并在评论家训练中引入重要性采样校正

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central component of large language model (LLM) post-training, yet token-level credit lacks a generally accepted mathematical definition, leaving its relationship to commonly used training signals unclear. We formulate three regularity conditions, namely Completeness, Prefix Consistency, and Neutrality, and prove that they uniquely determine token-level credit. This characterization provides a unified basis for explaining phenomena across existing algorithms and guides the development of an improved actor-critic training procedure. Through this lens, an ideal teacher in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) acts as an implicit critic, yielding an expected policy gradient proportional to that induced by token-level credit. Response-level REINFORCE Leave-One-Out (RLOO) signals match the expected policy-gradient contribution of token-level credit despite their coarser granularity. We further establish approximate credit sparsity under bounded outcome rewards and show how intermediate critic errors in Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) can become comparable to the underlying credit. These motivate Policy Aligned Critic Training (PACT), which adopts an Actor-then-Critic update order to apply importance sampling correction to critic training and better align the critic with the updated policy. In agentic mathematical reasoning, PACT achieves 72.87% average accuracy across four benchmarks, outperforming GRPO and PPO by 8.80 and 13.16 percentage points, respectively. On SWE-bench Verified, PACT achieves a pass rate of 67.4%, outperforming PPO, GRPO, and SAO by 2.4, 2.0, and 3.8 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry-Aware Hyperbolic Residual Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26342)
*Colombo, Alessio,Ayoughi, Melika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出几何感知的双曲残差量化，通过双曲残差聚合和折扣双曲直通估计器解决了双曲空间中残差量化的前向不一致和反向梯度不稳定问题。在推荐、层次化预测、图像tokenization和音频编码等任务上评估，表明其能改善层次化代码的稳定性与结构组织，但纯压缩场景下欧几里得RVQ仍占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双曲空间中扩展残差向量量化时，非结合性的双曲加法破坏了残差聚合的一致性，且标准直通估计器忽略潜在空间的几何结构，导致梯度传播不稳定和代码结构组织不佳。

Method: 论文提出了一种几何感知的双曲残差量化方法，整体架构围绕残差向量量化（RVQ）在Poincaré球模型上的扩展展开。输入为连续表示，经过多级残差量化器生成离散的多层token序列。前向传播中，每级残差通过双曲残差聚合进行组合，该聚合机制利用Poincaré球模型的性质，恢复了欧几里得空间中残差量化的可伸缩求和（telescoping）行为，确保多层残差能够一致地累加，从而形成稳定的层次化编码。反向传播中，采用折扣双曲直通估计器，将重构损失相对于量化输出的梯度视为一个整体几何块，通过一个带折扣因子的直通路径传回编码器，避免因逐级递归传递梯度而导致的几何不一致和数值不稳定。训练目标为最小化重构误差，同时隐式地鼓励代码的层次化组织。推理时，编码器输出连续表示，经多级双曲残差量化得到离散token序列；解码器利用这些token进行重构或下游任务。该方法在层次化预测、推荐、图像tokenization和神经音频编码任务上进行了评估，显示出更好的稳定性和结构组织，但同时也观察到结构-压缩权衡：欧几里得RVQ更适合纯压缩，而几何感知的双曲量化更适合层次化组织的离散潜在空间。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 双曲残差聚合：在Poincaré球上恢复残差量化的可伸缩求和行为。；折扣双曲直通估计器：将重构梯度作为单个几何块路由通过量化器，避免残差阶段间不稳定的递归梯度传输。

Abstract: Residual Vector Quantization turns continuous representations into discrete, multi-level token sequences. Yet most methods operate in Euclidean space, despite the coarse-to-fine structure of the resulting codes and the latent hierarchies present in many data domains. Hyperbolic geometry offers a natural alternative for hierarchical representations, but naive hyperbolic extensions introduce geometric inconsistencies: non-associative hyperbolic addition prevents consistent residual aggregation, while standard straight-through gradient estimation ignores the geometry of the latent space. We propose a geometry-aware hyperbolic residual quantization that addresses these issues in both the forward and backward passes. In the forward pass, Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation restores the telescoping behavior of residual quantization on the Poincare ball. In the backward pass, a discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator routes the reconstruction gradient through the quantizer as a single geometric block, avoiding unstable recursive gradient transport across residual stages. Evaluations on hierarchical prediction, recommendation, image tokenization, and neural audio coding tasks show that our method improves the stability and structural organization of hyperbolic residual codes over naive hyperbolic baselines. At the same time, we observe a clear structure-compression trade-off: Euclidean residual quantization remains preferable for pure compression, while geometry-aware hyperbolic quantization is most useful for hierarchically organized discrete latent spaces.

</details>


### [65] [CompKV: Compensation-Aware KV Selection for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26300)
*Huang, Zhen,Yao, Ruizhe,Liu, Danyi,Chen, Xinrui,Li, Shuwei,Zhong, Siru,Cao, Zijian,Lai, Yushan,Guo, Mingming,Zheng, Weijie,Fu, Haohuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CompKV 是首个补偿感知的稀疏注意力框架，通过理论分析块级均值补偿残差并据此选择 token 块，在长上下文推理中实现最高 6.85 倍自注意力加速，并在 RULER 与 LongBench-Pro 上优于对比稀疏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理中 KV cache 内存流量瓶颈，现有稀疏注意力方法将 token 选择与遗漏注意力尾部的补偿解耦，导致选择未考虑补偿误差，未能最小化总体近似误差。

Method: CompKV 是一种补偿感知的稀疏注意力框架。输入为长上下文序列的 token 表示，处理时先将 token 划分为多个块。核心模块包括：块级统计量提取模块，用于计算每个块的注意力质量和块内 logit 变化；补偿误差估计模块，基于理论分析得到块级均值补偿的残差上界，并用紧凑统计量近似该残差；选择模块，根据补偿误差估计优先选取那些若被遗漏将导致最大补偿误差的块，从而在给定预算下最小化整体近似误差。选中的块进行精确注意力计算，未选中的块采用均值补偿恢复其贡献。为实现高效部署，论文设计了异步实现，将选择与注意力计算流水线化以重叠开销。训练目标并非训练模型，而是优化选择准则以匹配下游补偿机制。推理流程为：对每个注意力头，先在线计算块级统计量，再依据补偿感知准则选择块，最后对选中块执行精确注意力并对未选块做均值补偿，输出注意力结果。整体架构通过显式耦合选择与补偿，解决了现有方法因解耦设计而忽略交互、导致补偿误差未被最小化的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "自注意力速度", "value": "最高 6.85×", "setting": "相对于 full attention，在长上下文推理场景下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出首个补偿感知的稀疏注意力框架 CompKV，将 token 分块并显式优化选择以最小化下游补偿机制留下的残差。；理论分析表明块级均值补偿的残差由块注意力质量与块内 logit 变化共同决定，并用紧凑的块级统计量近似该残差，得到可部署的选择准则。；设计高效异步实现，在 RULER 和 LongBench-Pro 上优于所评估的稀疏基线，实现最高 6.85 倍的自注意力加速。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models (LLMs) are bottlenecked by KV cache memory traffic during long-context inference. Sparse attention is widely used to accelerate LLM inference by computing exact attention over a selected subset of tokens. To recover the contribution of tokens excluded from exact attention, recent methods apply coarse-grained compensation to the omitted attention tail. However, existing methods typically select tokens based on attention mass and only then compensate for the unselected tokens. This decoupled design overlooks their interaction: selection should prioritize tokens that would leave the largest compensation error if omitted. To address this limitation, we introduce CompKV, the first compensation-aware sparse attention framework that divides tokens into blocks and explicitly optimizes selection for the downstream compensation mechanism. Our theoretical analysis shows that the residual left by block-level mean compensation is governed by both block attention mass and within-block logit variation. We approximate this residual using compact block-level statistics, yielding a deployable selection criterion. We further develop an efficient asynchronous implementation. Experiments on RULER and LongBench-Pro show that CompKV performs best among the evaluated sparse baselines while delivering up to a $6.85\times$ self-attention speedup over full attention.

</details>


### [66] [You Only Need 2/3 of the Chosen Experts: An Empirical Study of Dynamic Expert Pruning in Fine-Grained MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25809)
*Chen, Yuanteng,Lai, Qiwei,Tianqi, Chen,Wang, Peisong,Shao, Yuantian,Zeng, Nanxin,Liu, Zhilei,Li, Chuangyi,Liu, Jing,Cheng, Jian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统实证细粒度MoE LLM的动态专家剪枝，发现每token专家选择高度冗余：均匀保留约三分之二专家即可保留98.8%平均性能，并在两个服务后端获得1.2-1.7倍实测加速；最佳动态规则在保守预算下仅比均匀截断好不到1%，但在激进剪枝下可恢复至多3.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在细粒度MoE LLM中，每token选择的专家冗余程度如何、现有动态剪枝方法能否有效利用该冗余，以及模型对激进剪枝的敏感性由什么因素决定。

Method: 论文研究细粒度MoE LLM的推理路径：模型包含数百个专家，每个token经路由器选择若干专家参与前向计算。动态专家剪枝在路由选择后对每token的专家集合施加截断或分配规则，减少实际激活的专家数。论文比较的核心基线是均匀截断：对每个token被选专家集合按固定比例保留，例如约三分之二，从而在不改变模型权重和路由打分机制的前提下减少专家计算。动态方法则根据历史激活频率、路由概率或其他评分规则为不同token分配不同数量的专家。实验评估在十二个细粒度MoE检查点上进行，覆盖九个架构族，使用十一个基准衡量知识QA、数学、代码生成和通用推理。服务侧在两个后端上测量端到端加速，验证减少专家激活带来的实际推理收益。训练目标或优化未作为论文重点披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "保留性能", "value": "98.8%", "setting": "均匀保留约三分之二的被选专家，12个细粒度MoE检查点平均，11个基准"}, {"metric": "相对均匀截断的性能提升", "value": "最高3.0%", "setting": "激进剪枝预算下，最佳已发表动态规则，增益集中在生成任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对十二个细粒度MoE检查点、九个架构族进行系统实证，覆盖知识QA、数学、代码生成和通用推理共十一个基准。；发现均匀保留约三分之二的被选专家即可平均保留98.8%的未剪枝性能，仅需改一个整数，并在两个服务后端实现1.2-1.7倍实测加速。；在匹配专家预算下，最佳已发表动态规则与均匀截断基线差距不到1%，说明保守预算下动态分配空间很小；但在激进剪枝下最佳规则可相对均匀截断恢复至多3.0%。；揭示剪枝敏感性依赖模型特性：更大模型和thinking模型更鲁棒，多模态模型更脆弱。

Abstract: Fine-grained mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures have become a mainstream design for open-weight LLMs, with hundreds of experts and increasingly many selected per token. This shift makes dynamic expert pruning an attractive route to cheaper inference. Yet existing evidence comes largely from coarser architectures and likelihood-scored multiple-choice benchmarks, leaving three central questions open in the fine-grained regime: how redundant per-token expert selection is, how effectively existing pruning methods exploit that redundancy, and what governs a model's sensitivity to pruning. We fill this gap with a systematic empirical study of twelve fine-grained MoE checkpoints spanning nine architecture families, with a core suite of eleven benchmarks covering knowledge QA, mathematics, code generation, and general reasoning. We find that expert selection is far more redundant than the field's operating points assume: uniformly retaining about two thirds of the selected experts preserves 98.8% of unpruned performance on average, requiring only a one-integer change and delivering 1.2-1.7x measured speedup across two serving backends. This simple baseline leaves little room for dynamic allocation at conservative budgets: even the best published rules differ from it by under 1% at matched expert budgets. Their value emerges under aggressive pruning, where the best rules recover up to 3.0% over uniform truncation, with gains concentrated in the generative tasks that suffer the sharpest degradation. Sensitivity to aggressive pruning also depends on the model: larger and thinking models are more resilient, whereas multimodal models are more vulnerable. Together, these findings reveal how much expert computation fine-grained MoEs can dispense with, and establish when dynamic allocation earns its complexity, informing both practical deployment and future pruning methods.

</details>


### [67] [From Experts to Sub-experts: Fine-grained Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25655)
*Tan, Zhentao,Liu, Chang,Liu, Yao,Wu, Yue,Ye, Jieping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NSFT 把 MoE LLM 的参数高效微调粒度从整个专家细化到专家内部的结构化通道组（子专家），以路由重要性与专家内部激活显著性联合选择任务相关子专家，并用学习率缩放与动态梯度缩放补偿稀疏更新带来的更新幅度下降。在 OLMoE 与 Ling-mini-2.0 上的领域任务与通用基准实验中，NSFT 以更少可训练参数稳定优于代表性 PEFT 与专家级稀疏微调基线，方向对 MoE 模型的训练系统有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 MoE LLM 中，现有 PEFT（如 LoRA 基于预定义权重矩阵）和专家级稀疏微调（更新整个被选中专家）的适配粒度仍然过粗：被激活的专家内部实际上高度稀疏，只有小部分中间通道对下游任务强响应，因此需要把微调粒度从专家细化到子专家（sub-expert）。

Method: NSFT 面向 MoE LLM 的微调流程：输入为下游任务数据，前向传播沿用原始 MoE 路由，得到每个 token 的被激活专家及其路由权重。与 LoRA 在预定义权重矩阵上插入低秩分支不同，NSFT 在每个被激活专家的 intermediate 维度上做结构分解，将中间通道划分为若干结构化的通道组，每个通道组视为一个子专家（sub-expert）。选择模块综合两类信号：(1) 路由重要性，即该专家被路由分配的权重或频率所反映的重要性；(2) 专家内部激活显著性，即前向激活中中间通道对当前任务的响应强度。二者结合后挑出任务相关的子专家，仅对这些子专家的对应参数进行稀疏部分更新，未选中的通道组保持冻结，从而实现比专家级更细的适配粒度。由于稀疏更新使被更新参数的有效更新幅度变小，NSFT 在优化阶段引入学习率缩放，对被选子树相关的学习率进行放大以补偿，同时使用动态梯度缩放，根据稀疏结构动态调整梯度尺度，稳定并加速稀疏部分更新下的收敛。训练目标仍为下游任务的常规微调损失，只是把可训练参数限制在被选中的子专家通道组上，并配套缩放策略。推理阶段不需要改变模型主体结构，使用微调后的专家通道参数即可，但由于论文未披露推理时对路由、合并或显存的具体处理方式，此处不做推断。该设计解决的问题是：专家级稀疏微调粒度仍偏粗，而细粒度到通道组能够以更少的可训练参数实现更精确的任务适配。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游领域任务与通用基准上的综合表现", "value": "一致优于代表性 PEFT 与专家级稀疏微调基线，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数，并保持有竞争力的通用能力", "setting": "OLMoE 与 Ling-mini-2.0 模型；挑战性领域特定任务与通用基准的离线评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 NSFT（Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning），在 MoE 专家的 intermediate 维度上将其分解为结构化通道组（structured channel groups），形成子专家单元。；子专家选择同时结合路由重要性（routing importance）与专家内部激活显著性（intra-expert activation saliency）进行任务相关的稀疏参数选择。；针对稀疏部分更新导致的等效更新幅度下降，引入学习率缩放（learning-rate scaling）与动态梯度缩放（dynamic gradient scaling）进行补偿。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) scale rapidly, dense full-parameter adaptation becomes increasingly expensive, motivating sparse and modular architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This shift raises a key question for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT): at what granularity should parameters be selected and updated? Existing PEFT methods such as LoRA operate on predefined weight matrices, while expert-level sparse tuning methods update entire selected experts. However, we observe that activated experts are internally sparse, with only a small fraction of intermediate channels strongly responding to downstream tasks, indicating that expert-level adaptation is still too coarse. We propose NSFT (Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning), a fine-grained PEFT framework that refines MoE adaptation from experts to sub-experts. NSFT decomposes each expert along the intermediate dimension into structured channel groups and selects task-relevant sub-experts by combining routing importance with intra-expert activation saliency. To optimize sparse partial updates, NSFT further introduces learning-rate scaling and dynamic gradient scaling to compensate for the reduced effective update magnitude. Experiments on OLMoE and Ling-mini-2.0 across challenging domain-specific tasks and general benchmarks show that NSFT consistently outperforms representative PEFT and expert-level sparse tuning baselines, while using substantially fewer trainable parameters and preserving competitive general capability. These results suggest that sub-expert-level adaptation is a more precise and efficient PEFT paradigm for MoE LLMs.

</details>


### [68] [Terminal Shrinkage Averaging Reveals a Schedule-Estimator Interaction in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25482)
*Ousherovitch, Adam,Wang, Yixin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)，通过在原始最终迭代和近期检查点平均之间插值，解耦学习率调度与最终模型估计器。理论分析揭示了调度与估计器的交互，并在 NanoChat 实验中验证了组合方法能提升验证质量、加速 time-to-GPT-2 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 预训练默认返回原始最终迭代，将学习率调度与最终模型估计器（如原始最终迭代或检查点平均）耦合，导致调度同时影响优化进度和终端方差。本文旨在解耦这两个选择，在保持后期优化进度的同时降低返回模型的方差。

Method: TSA 方法：在预训练结束时，通过插值系数 α 将原始最终迭代与最近 K 个检查点的平均值结合，返回模型参数 θ_TSA = α * θ_final + (1-α) * (1/K sum_{i=1}^K θ_{final-i+1})。学习率调度采用终端学习率调度（terminal LR schedule），在局部二次近似下分析表明，TSA 会改变最优的终端学习率衰减曲线。训练流程：使用选定的调度训练 LLM，保存最近检查点，然后应用 TSA 生成部署模型。实验在 NanoChat 上进行，包括控制变量实验和 depth-22 模型验证。推理时直接使用 TSA 生成的模型参数，无额外开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "validation quality", "value": "improved", "setting": "depth-22 NanoChat, combined schedule and estimator"}, {"metric": "time-to-GPT-2", "value": "faster than public baseline", "setting": "qualifying run"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)，在原始最终迭代和近期检查点平均之间进行插值，以平衡近期进度和终端方差。；在局部二次近似下理论分析 TSA 如何改变首选终端学习率调度。；通过一系列受控 NanoChat 实验验证调度与估计器的交互作用。；将发现的组合调度和估计器迁移到深度 22 的 NanoChat，提升验证质量，并在 qualifying time-to-GPT-2 运行中加速基准。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) pretraining conventionally returns the raw final iterate. This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training while reducing variation in the returned model. To this end, we propose \emph{Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)}, which interpolates between the raw final iterate and the average of recent checkpoints to balance recent progress against terminal variation. We analyze how TSA changes the preferred terminal learning-rate schedule under a local quadratic approximation and test this interaction through a sequence of controlled NanoChat experiments. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting gains transfer to depth-22 NanoChat, where the combined schedule and estimator improve validation quality. A qualifying time-to-GPT-2 run also finishes faster than the public baseline used in our experiments, providing preliminary evidence of benchmark acceleration.

</details>


### [69] [Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25433)
*Girija, Sanjay Surendranath,Nath, Aniruddh,Wei, Li,Jiang, Yanhao,Andrews, Shawn,Heldt, Lukasz,Wu, Yi,Mahajan, Aditya,Sharma, Mohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Google/YouTube 提出 Light Heads 框架，通过 stop-gradient 和无状态每日训练在现有生产多任务排序模型上动态注入新任务，无需重训练骨干模型，将多任务实验迭代周期从数周缩短至数天，并已在 YouTube 规模部署并产生在线 A/B 实验收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超大规模多任务排序模型中引入新预测任务时，存在任务冲突风险、骨干模型与下游模型重训练成本高、奖励组合公式调参耗时，导致实验速度慢（数周级）。

Method: Light Heads 框架部署于连续在线学习环境。该框架在现有已训练的多任务排序模型上动态添加轻量级排序头。每个新任务对应一个独立的轻量级头，其输入来自骨干模型的共享表示，但通过 stop-gradient 阻断新任务梯度回传至骨干模型，从而严格隔离新任务，避免与已有任务产生冲突。新任务头采用无状态每日训练（stateless daily training），即每日从零开始训练该头（冷启动），仅利用当天的训练数据，无需骨干模型重训练或下游模型调整。集中式配置管理允许将 Light Heads 同时添加到多个模型中，使得新任务产生的预测可以快速用于生成训练数据，并解锁下游模型的联合训练。整体流程为：骨干模型保持冻结，新任务头以无状态方式每日更新，通过配置注入多个模型，从而支持快速 A/B 实验和部署。在推理时，新任务头与骨干模型共享表示计算路径，额外计算开销极小。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "迭代周期", "value": "从数周缩短至数天", "setting": "YouTube 规模生产环境多任务实验"}]}

Conclusion: 轻量级排序头（Light Heads）框架，支持动态注入新任务而无需冷启动或重训练骨干模型；采用 stop-gradient 和无状态每日训练隔离新任务，避免对现有任务产生负向干扰；集中式配置使 Light Heads 可同时添加到多个模型，加速训练数据生成和下游模型联合训练

Abstract: Modern production-scale recommender systems rely on complex, multi-task ranking models. Introducing new prediction tasks into these massive systems often causes bottlenecks - it risks negative task conflicts with existing tasks, and can lead to long development and experimentation cycles due to the expensive retraining of backbone models and downstream models or tuning of reward combination formulas. To address the critical challenge of slow experimentation velocity, we introduce the Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) framework. Designed for continuous online learning environments, Light Heads enable the dynamic injection of new tasks into existing multi-task ranking models, effectively obviating the need for model cold-starting and retraining of backbone models. By utilizing stop-gradients and stateless daily training, this design strictly isolates new tasks, mitigating the risk of adverse task conflicts. Crucially, this framework uses a centralized configuration that allows Light Heads to be added to multiple models simultaneously, unblocking faster training data generation and co-training of downstream models. Successfully deployed at YouTube scale, this approach reduces the iteration cycle for multi-task experimentation from several weeks to days. In this paper, we detail the system architecture, analyze the training dynamics of stateless cold-started heads, compare their performance to full heads, and demonstrate how Light Heads have enabled the rapid A/B experimentation and deployment of new ranking tasks that yield measurable production value.

</details>


### [70] [Trains but Doesn't Learn: A Post-Training Delivery Benchmark for LLM Agents as Forward-Deployed Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25237)
*Ding, Weihang,Zhan, Junfei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个针对LLM智能体作为前向部署工程师（FDE）的后训练交付基准。在受治理的交付平面上，智能体驱动十个阶段，oracle按阶段评分，揭示“训练但不学习”的静默失败，并设计验收门和检测器。实验在L40S/A100/H200上对8B-70B模型运行四个前沿智能体和人类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法回答LLM智能体能否被信任在FDE角色中完成端到端模型交付，尤其是“训练但不学习”（TBDL）的静默失败——损失下降、所有信号正常，但交付的模型与基座模型相比无改进。

Method: 论文构建了一个受治理的交付平面，其中LLM智能体扮演前向部署工程师（FDE），执行从数据接收到模型部署的十个阶段。每个阶段由oracle根据平台记录的事实进行客观评分。系统引入操作员运行的验收门，在付款前自动检查是否存在“训练但不学习”（TBDL）的失败——即训练损失下降但模型性能未超过基座模型。此外，一个基于已知损坏运行校准的检测器可在训练过程中标记严重损坏。实验涵盖了四个前沿智能体（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-luna、Gemini 3.7 Flash、DeepSeek V4-Pro），在计量L40S、A100和H200 GPU上端到端运行，覆盖8B至70B开源基座模型，并包括一个人类FDE基线在同一oracle下比较。所有场景在评分前均经过认证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 受治理的交付平面：智能体驱动十个阶段，oracle根据平台记录的事实对每个阶段评分。；发现并定义“训练但不学习”（TBDL）静默失败模式。；操作员运行的验收门可在付款前捕获所有此类运行。；在已知损坏运行上校准的检测器可在运行中标记严重损坏。

Abstract: Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.

</details>


### [71] [Latest Exact Match Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25802)
*BrÃ¶samle, Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEMA注意力机制，通过二值化查询/键并仅关注最近精确匹配，实现高效长上下文检索。理论证明其与word-RAM计算等价，并提出训练方法。在合成任务和834M参数语言模型上验证，推理时使用字典实现常数生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何设计一种注意力机制，使其在保持高效计算和内存的同时，能够像word-RAM一样进行通用计算，并支持长上下文场景下的精确检索。

Method: LEMA是一种注意力变体，其中查询和键被二值化，每个查询仅关注最近精确匹配的键。这导致非可微操作。训练时，作者使用直通估计器处理二值化，并引入一个软注意力代理，在训练过程中退火至LEMA的硬注意力。模型在合成关联召回任务上训练，并扩展到834M参数的语言模型。推理时，采用基于字典的实现，将键值对存储在字典中，字典驻留主内存，从而支持与上下文长度无关的常数生成速度。理论分析表明，LEMA transformers with chain of thought可以模拟word-RAM，反之word-RAM也可以以每令牌独立于上下文长度的成本模拟LEMA transformers。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "关联召回性能", "value": "优于gated DeltaNet (GDN) 固定状态大小", "setting": "合成关联召回任务"}, {"metric": "语言模型损失", "value": "匹配约一半大小的softmax transformer", "setting": "高达834M参数的缩放测试"}, {"metric": "重复罕见短语和针检索任务", "value": "落后于softmax transformer，但召回距离长于类似大小的GDN模型", "setting": "语言模型评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Latest Exact Match Attention (LEMA)，查询和键二值化，每个查询只关注最近精确匹配的键。；理论证明LEMA transformers（带思维链）可以模拟word-RAM，且word-RAM可以以与上下文长度无关的每令牌成本模拟LEMA transformers，建立了双向计算等价。；提出训练LEMA的方法：对二值化使用直通估计器，并采用软注意力代理退火至LEMA。；实现基于字典的推理，将字典存储在主内存而非VRAM，实现常数生成速度。

Abstract: We introduce latest exact match attention (LEMA), an attention variant for transformers where queries and keys are binarized and each query attends only to the latest exactly matching key. We prove that LEMA transformers with chain of thought can simulate word-RAMs, as was recently shown for the less restrictive rightmost hard attention. In contrast to prior hard attention variants, the restriction to exact matches enables an efficient converse direction: word-RAMs can simulate LEMA transformers at a cost per token independent of the context length. Together, these results yield a close correspondence between the two computational models in terms of both compute and memory. Beyond the theory, we propose a training method for LEMA transformers that handles their non-differentiable operations with a straight-through estimator for the binarization and a soft attention surrogate annealed towards LEMA. On a synthetic associative recall task, LEMA models trained this way use their growing state to store and recall a large number of associations, outperforming gated DeltaNet (GDN) with its fixed state size. As a first scaling test, we train LEMA language models with up to 834 million parameters. They match softmax transformers of around half their size in loss and, on repeated rare phrases and a needle-retrieval task, remain behind softmax transformers but recall across longer distances than GDN models of comparable size. Finally, we implement dictionary-based inference for LEMA transformers and show constant generation speed comparable to GDN despite their growing state, with the dictionaries residing in main memory rather than VRAM. Code is available at https://github.com/moritzbroe/latest_exact_match_attention.

</details>


### [72] [MAGIC: Mixed-Granularity Agent Graphs via Incremental Construction with Dense-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26667)
*Yang, Kairui,Yi, Ziheng,Li, Xunkai,An, Minghao,Liu, Zhanke,Chen, Zekai,Li, Rong-Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAGIC提出一种混合粒度智能体图构建框架，通过稠密奖励强化学习为每个功能角色局部选择单智能体或可复用组，在八个基准上超越SOTA并展现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作拓扑生成方法在整个组织中统一使用个体智能体或预定义组，忽略了不同子任务对协作粒度的差异化需求，且组合构建空间大、中间反馈稀疏，难以有效学习。

Method: MAGIC将多智能体组织构建形式化为混合粒度图生成问题。输入为任务描述，构建过程顺序进行：每一步选择一个功能角色，将该角色实例化为单个智能体或一个可复用智能体组，并将其连接到当前已构建的图单元中，最终形成任务特定的协作拓扑。构建策略由强化学习直接优化，训练时从当前策略采样构建轨迹，以轨迹回报作为目标，同时引入基于势能的奖励塑形项——由探测效用和结构信号提供中间反馈，并与最终答案奖励共同构成稠密奖励，且塑形不改变最优策略。推理时使用学习到的策略为任务生成图，然后按该图执行多智能体协作完成推理。论文声称在八个基准上超越SOTA，并在效率研究中展示强推理效率，但未披露具体参数规模、训练资源或推理资源细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出按功能角色局部选择粒度的混合粒度智能体图构建方法，同时结合细粒度控制与可复用协作模式。；设计顺序构建策略：依次选择功能角色、实例化为单智能体或可复用组，并连接到已有单元。；提出稠密奖励强化学习框架，直接使用当前策略采样轨迹的回报优化构建策略。；采用基于势能的奖励塑形，利用探测效用和结构信号提供中间反馈，同时保持累积任务奖励不变。

Abstract: Collaboration topology shapes both the performance and execution cost of LLM-based multi-agent systems. Because tasks differ in complexity and required capabilities, recent approaches generate task-specific collaboration graphs that specify agent participation and information flow. However, representative topology generators use either individual agents or predefined groups throughout an organization, overlooking differing collaboration needs across subtasks. Our key insight is to select granularity locally for each functional role, combining fine-grained control with reusable collaboration patterns within one organization. Learning such organizations requires exploring a combinatorial construction space with limited intermediate feedback from final-answer rewards. Therefore, we propose MAGIC, a dense-reward reinforcement learning framework for mixed-granularity graph generation. Specifically, MAGIC constructs a mixed-granularity agent graph by sequentially selecting a functional role, instantiating it as a single agent or reusable group, and connecting it to existing units. We directly optimize the construction policy using returns from trajectories sampled under the current policy and use potential-based reward shaping to provide intermediate feedback from probe-based utility and structural signals while preserving the cumulative task reward. MAGIC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across eight benchmarks and demonstrates strong inference efficiency in our efficiency study.

</details>


### [73] [Disaggregated Quantization: Specializing LLM Prefill and Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26333)
*Panferov, Andrei,Kleinegger, Maximilian,Priyadarshi, Sweta,Blankevoort, Tijmen,Alistarh, Dan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出disaggregated quantization，为LLM prefill与decode阶段分别特化量化格式、权重与存储位置；在Qwen 3和Gemma 3上移除decode激活量化提升decode任务精度且不增加成本，训练NVFP4 prefiller使1-bit精度在MMLU-Pro提升32.5、MMMU-Pro提升35.3，ODP在27B模型8K prompt下TTFT加速1.78x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prefill与decode阶段对量化格式的需求不同：低精度算术加速prompt处理，紧凑权重减少生成时的内存流量，单一量化配置难以同时优化两阶段

Method: 论文提出disaggregated quantization（DQ），将LLM推理的prefill与decode阶段视为具有不同量化需求的独立目标。输入为prompt与已生成token；在prefill阶段使用compute-native低精度算术（如NVFP4）加速prompt处理，并可使用单独训练的prefill权重checkpoint；在decode阶段保留更高精度或移除activation量化，仅使用紧凑权重以减少内存流量。两个阶段可共享部分权重格式或使用独立checkpoint。为在单设备上容纳额外checkpoint，ODP将prefill权重从SSD流式加载，并把加载开销按prompt长度摊销。训练目标为在给定量化格式下恢复或提升任务精度，通过post-training quantization与单独的prefill权重训练实现。推理路径在vLLM与llama.cpp中实现，prefill阶段调度NVFP4 prefiller，decode阶段调度GGUF decoder。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "1-bit accuracy on MMLU-Pro", "value": "32.5 points", "setting": "Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoder + NVFP4 prefiller，未修改decode checkpoint"}, {"metric": "1-bit accuracy on MMMU-Pro", "value": "35.3 points", "setting": "Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoder + NVFP4 prefiller，未修改decode checkpoint"}, {"metric": "time-to-first-token speedup", "value": "1.78x", "setting": "27B模型，8K prompt长度，llama.cpp，ODP vs weight-only baseline"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Disaggregated quantization（DQ）：为prefill与decode分别特化计算格式、权重与存储位置；在decode阶段移除activation量化以提升decode-heavy任务精度而不增加推理成本；训练独立的compute-native prefill权重，加速prompt处理并匹配或超过weight-only推理精度；Offloaded disaggregated prefill（ODP）：将额外prefill checkpoint权重从SSD流式加载，按prompt长度摊销加载开销；在vLLM中评估disaggregated serving下的精度，并通过PTQ在最高2.8T参数模型上验证shared-weight格式分解

Abstract: Prefill and decode reward different approaches to quantization: low-precision arithmetic accelerates prompt processing, while compact weights reduce memory traffic during generation. We propose "disaggregated quantization" (DQ), which specializes computation formats, weights and storage placement to both of these phases. On Qwen 3 and Gemma 3, removing activation quantization specifically on decode improves accuracy on decode-heavy tasks without increasing inference cost. Training separate compute-native prefill weights accelerates prompt processing relative to weight-only inference while matching or exceeding its accuracy at 2-3-bit decode on both decode-heavy and prefill-heavy tasks. With released Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoders, training an NVFP4 prefiller improves 1-bit accuracy by 32.5 points on MMLU-Pro and 35.3 on MMMU-Pro without modifying the decode checkpoint. To accommodate the additional checkpoint on a single device, offloaded disaggregated prefill (ODP) streams its weights from SSD, amortizing loading over prompt length. On the same 27B model, ODP delivers a 1.78x time-to-first-token speedup over the weight-only baseline at 8K prompt length in llama.cpp. We evaluate accuracy under disaggregated serving in vLLM and further validate shared-weight format disaggregation through post-training quantization on models up to 2.8T parameters.

</details>


### [74] [MSA-CITE: A Co-Adapted LoRA Specialist Ecology for Fixed-Budget Small-Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26217)
*Li, Ruitong,Guo, Binjie,Mo, Aisheng,Su, Guowei,Li, Jie,Zhang, Ru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MSA-CITE，通过组合多个后训练LoRA分支并在推理时校准选择，在固定四次生成预算下提升小模型数学推理准确率，在多个测试集上优于单分支基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定推理预算（仅允许生成四次）下，如何利用多个后训练得到的LoRA分支（专家）来提升小模型（Qwen3-4B）的推理准确率，而非仅依赖单一检查点重复采样。

Method: 该方法基于Qwen3-4B主干，保留四个冻结的LoRA分支，每个分支来自不同的后训练轨迹。在推理时，对于每个输入，从每个分支采样一个完成（共四次生成）。然后，将生成的终结答案分组为等价类，对每个类计算校准派生的源先验之和作为分数，并采用确定性平局规则选择代表答案。该读出阶段不学习评估结果，也不引入额外生成、验证器或重排序步骤。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "65.5%", "setting": "200个保留数学题，四路径组合 vs 最强单分支基线62.0%"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "42.0%", "setting": "100个主题不相交偏移集，四路径组合 vs 单分支基线40.0%"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将多个被丢弃的后训练LoRA检查点视为可组合的部署资产，形成专家生态。；提出MSA-CITE方法：对多个分支生成的答案进行等价类分组，利用校准的先验分数进行选择，并采用确定性平局打破规则。；固定每分支生成一次，总预算仍为四次，不增加额外生成、验证器或重排序。

Abstract: Compact language models are typically deployed by retaining a single post-training checkpoint and sampling it repeatedly. In this work, we challenge this practice by treating multiple discarded checkpoints as composable assets for deployment. Starting from a single Qwen3-4B backbone, we preserve four frozen LoRA branches, each derived from a different post-training trajectory. stead of drawing four generations from one branch, we allocate a fixed four-generation budget by sampling one completion from each branch. Our method, Multi-path Specialist Adaptation with Calibrated Inference-Time Evidence (MSA-CITE), processes the resulting portfolio by grouping terminal answers into equivalence classes, scoring each class via summed calibration-derived source priors, and selecting a representative under deterministic tie-breaking rules. The readout stage does not learn from evaluation results, nor does it introduce additional generations, verifiers, or reranking steps. On 200 held-out mathematics items, the four-path portfolio achieves 65.5% accuracy, compared with 62.0% for the strongest single-branch baseline. On a 100-item subject-disjoint shift, it attains 42.0% versus 40.0%. Under in-distribution conditions, the improvements over homogeneous SFT and Online-OPD repetition are robust; results against the strongest baseline and under shifted conditions are not conclusive. Our findings offer a narrow but concrete contribution: post-training branches, even without co-training, can be collectively beneficial for deployment.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Imitation: Auditing the Recoverability of Reasoning in Distilled Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26216)
*Li, Ruitong,Guo, Binjie,Mo, Aisheng,Su, Guowei,Wang, Han,Li, Jie,Zhang, Ru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出前缀恢复指标来审计蒸馏模型对教师正解的持续推理能力，并将其与 CE 和 reverse-KL 梯度冲突关联。在 Qwen3 0.6B-8B 教师-学生对上发现：学生容量小于2B时 reverse-KL 对数学和代码最有效；固定1000条轨迹，学生从0.6B增大到4B，平均前缀恢复从71.0%升至91.9%，鲁棒-脆弱差距从46.0降至14.4百分点；教师固定8B时冲突分离度从0.993降至0.233。该诊断有助于选择昂贵的分布级蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何度量教师生成的正解对学生而言是否可继续（可恢复），并解释 reverse-KL 与 cross-entropy 蒸馏目标冲突如何随学生容量变化，从而指导分布级蒸馏的选择。

Method: 论文未披露具体模型架构，Qwen3 教师-学生对为已有模型。方法流程：给定教师生成的正解轨迹，在推理过程的 25%、50%、75% 前缀位置截断，让学生模型续写并评估最终答案是否正确，得到前缀恢复率。同时计算全词表上 cross-entropy 梯度与 reverse-KL 梯度的余弦相似度，定义为冲突分离度，用以量化两个目标在参数更新方向上的不一致程度。在相邻 Qwen3 学生-教师对上系统变化学生容量，观察恢复率、冲突分离度以及蒸馏效果的变化，形成容量依赖的迁移区域分析。目标干预实验通过独立地改变损失目标，测量 reverse-KL 对脆弱轨迹（恢复率低的轨迹）的挽救效果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均前缀恢复率", "value": "71.0% → 91.9%", "setting": "固定1000条轨迹，学生规模从0.6B增至4B"}, {"metric": "鲁棒-脆弱恢复差距", "value": "46.0 → 14.4 百分点", "setting": "学生规模从0.6B增至4B"}, {"metric": "冲突分离度", "value": "0.993 → 0.233", "setting": "教师固定为8B，学生规模从0.6B增至4B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出前缀恢复（prefix recovery）指标：在已验证解的 25%/50%/75% 处揭示前缀，评估学生能否正确完成剩余推理，作为蒸馏兼容性的实用诊断。；将恢复能力与全词表上 cross-entropy 和 reverse-KL 梯度的余弦冲突联系起来，并定义冲突分离度。；在相邻 Qwen3 教师-学生对（0.6B-8B）上系统测量容量依赖的迁移区域，发现 reverse-KL 在小于2B的学生上数学与代码改进最一致。；通过独立的目标干预实验发现 reverse-KL 对脆弱轨迹的挽救最大。

Abstract: A correct teacher solution becomes useful supervision when the receiving student can continue its reasoning. We measure this compatibility with prefix recovery: after revealing 25%, 50%, or 75% of a verified solution, we test whether the student completes it correctly. We connect recovery to the cosine conflict between cross-entropy and reverse-KL gradients over the full vocabulary. Across adjacent Qwen3 teacher-student pairs from 0.6B to 8B parameters, reverse-KL distillation delivers its most consistent mathematical and code improvements for the two students below 2B parameters. On a fixed cohort of 1,000 trajectories, average prefix recovery rises from 71.0% to 91.9% as student size increases from 0.6B to 4B, and the robust-fragile recovery gap contracts from 46.0 to 14.4 percentage points. With the teacher fixed at 8B, conflict separation falls from 0.993 to 0.233. An independent objective intervention finds the largest reverse-KL rescue on fragile trajectories. The three measurements locate the same capacity-dependent transfer regime: distribution matching has the greatest headroom when correct traces remain unevenly recoverable. Prefix recovery provides a practical diagnostic for selecting costly distribution-level distillation.

</details>


### [76] [Spectral Tail Interventions in Decoder-Only Language Models: Reasoning-Sensitive Weight Structure from Controlled Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26165)
*Shihab, Ibne Farabi,Akhter, Sanjida,Swaqeeb, Md Najmus,Ahsan, Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al,Sharma, Anuj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过受控干预研究decoder-only语言模型权重矩阵的谱尾部集中现象，发现QK乘积尾部编辑比谱匹配对照更损害推理性能，并证明乘积目标分解能更好保留尾部子空间；同时显示逆参与率先于准确率变化，提出尾部感知LoRA加速微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间中的谱尾部集中现象是否真正参与语言模型计算，而不仅是相关性？

Method: 论文针对decoder-only Transformer的权重矩阵进行谱分析。首先，在固定相对偏移下，推导有限宽度条件界，将权重矩阵平方奇异值的逆参与率(IPR)与中心预softmax logit的峰度联系起来，表明谱尾部集中与logit分布形状存在理论关联。然后，定义逐点查询-键(QK)乘积尾部目标，即对每对query-key的点积的尾部进行干预；并设计乘积目标分解，直接对QK乘积矩阵进行低秩分解，以保持原生注意力计算路径。与此对比，独立因子手术分别对Q和K因子进行低秩截断或修改。实验在三个基础检查点五个推理基准上进行，通过比较学习到的尾部编辑与五个固定谱匹配Haar对照的均值，评估编辑对推理性能的损害。此外，对指令微调的Phi检查点单独分析。组件隔离实验识别QK、value-output和多层感知机(MLP)块的贡献，但理论仅覆盖QK。在单独研究中，观察逆参与率在池化准确率转变之前发生变化，并探索残差化尾部感知LoRA，调整LoRA的初始化或更新以关注尾部子空间，从而在更少训练步数内达到目标分数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "显著配对对比数", "value": "18/20", "setting": "3个基础检查点×5个推理基准，经Holm校正后显著；另2个方向一致但不显著"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 推导有限宽度条件界，将平方奇异值的逆参与率与中心预softmax logit峰度关联；定义逐点查询-键(QK)乘积尾部目标，并设计乘积目标分解以保持原生注意力计算；在多个模型检查点和推理基准上进行受控干预实验，比较因子手术与乘积目标分解；发现逆参与率在池化准确率转变之前发生变化；提出残差化尾部感知LoRA，相比标准LoRA和PiSSA更早达到目标分数

Abstract: Weight-space structure often correlates with language-model behavior, but correlation alone does not establish computational involvement. We study concentrated upper spectral tails in decoder-only transformers through controlled interventions. At a fixed relative offset, we derive a finite-width conditional bound linking the inverse participation ratio of squared singular values to central pre-softmax logit kurtosis. We then define a pointwise query--key ($QK$) product-tail target and compare independent factor surgery with a product-targeted factorization that preserves native attention computation. Across three base checkpoints and five reasoning benchmarks, plus an instruction-tuned Phi checkpoint analyzed separately, the learned-tail edit is more damaging than the mean of five fixed spectrum-matched Haar controls in all 20 model--task cells. Eighteen paired contrasts remain significant after Holm correction, while two are directional but inconclusive. Product-targeted factors attain higher held-out tail-subspace fractions, providing an empirical bridge between product- and factor-level interventions. Component isolation identifies contributions from $QK$, value--output, and multilayer-perceptron blocks, although the theorem covers only $QK$. In separate studies, inverse participation precedes pooled accuracy transitions under a matched crossing rule, and residualized tail-aware low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reaches targets earlier than standard LoRA and PiSSA while final-score intervals overlap. Conclusions are restricted to the evaluated checkpoints, layers, tasks, interventions, and controls.

</details>


### [77] [Block-Level Weight-Space Structure Persists Under Post-Training: An Empirical Study Across LLM Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26147)
*Wang, Zhaohui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 实证研究揭示后训练虽改变所有参数但保留块级权重空间结构，据此设计LinkerLLM惰性加载器，可跨变体共享块降低GPU显存18-48%，在24GB消费级GPU上运行最多5个7B变体，并报告了质量保持的透明评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练如何改变LLM权重空间几何，以及能否利用权重块级结构实现跨变体的内存共享以减少部署开销。

Method: 论文对四个架构家族（Qwen2.5、Llama-3.1/3.2、Mistral、Gemma-2）的八个配置进行实证研究，分析后训练（如指令微调、对话微调）对预训练权重空间的影响。输入为同一基座模型产生的多个后训练变体（base, instruct, chat, code）的权重张量。核心模块包括：1) 权重空间几何分析：逐张量比较（哈希、余弦相似度、相对Frobenius距离），发现所有张量均被修改但块级几何保持稳定；2) 独立性分析：对比独立训练的特化模型，发现权重空间不连通；3) LinkerLLM惰性加载器：在加载多个变体时，通过检测块级相似性，将可共享的块在内存中别名化，仅加载差异块，从而减少GPU显存占用。推理时，加载器按需加载并共享块，实现内存高效的多变体服务。评估在MMLU、ARC-Challenge、HellaSwag、WinoGrande基准上进行，衡量共享后质量下降。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "GPU显存节省", "value": "18-48%", "setting": "共享块别名加载多个共存变体"}, {"metric": "单卡24GB可容纳7B参数变体数量", "value": "最多5个", "setting": "24GB消费级GPU"}, {"metric": "质量保持（MMLU, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, WinoGrande）", "value": "8个配置中5个保留至少94%的无共享变体质量；剩余3个中每个有1个基准低于阈值（87-91%）", "setting": "块共享决策下的评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现后训练会导致全部张量发生变化（0%哈希去重节省），但块级结构保持高度相似（平均余弦相似度>0.99，相对Frobenius距离<0.13），形成粒度差距。；识别出独立训练的特化模型（如Qwen2.5-Coder）与通用基座的余弦相似度仅约0.64，表明权重空间中存在不连通的区域。；提出LinkerLLM惰性加载器，通过别名共享可共享块，在多个共存变体间实现GPU显存节省。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are deployed as families of post-trained variants (base, instruct, chat, code) derived from a shared set of pre-trained weights. We present an empirical study of how post-training transforms weight-space geometry, covering eight configurations across four architecture families (Qwen2.5, Llama-3.1/3.2, Mistral, Gemma-2). We identify a granularity gap: post-training modifies every tensor (zero of 291-339 tensors remain byte-identical, so hash-based deduplication achieves 0% savings), yet preserves block-level structure (mean cosine similarity exceeds 0.99 and relative Frobenius distance stays below 0.13). Post-training therefore acts as a structured perturbation that shifts every parameter while leaving block-level geometry intact. The property is not universal: independently trained specializations (for example, Qwen2.5-Coder) attain cosine similarity around 0.64 with the general base, indicating a disconnected region of weight space. Perturbation magnitude varies systematically with model scale, architecture family, and post-training recipe. As a practical application, we build LinkerLLM, a lazy loader that aliases shareable blocks across co-resident variants, achieving 18-48% GPU memory savings and enabling up to five 7B-parameter variants on a single 24 GB consumer GPU. Five of eight configurations retain at least 94% of the unshared variant's quality on MMLU, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, and WinoGrande; the remaining three (Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B) have one below-threshold benchmark each (87-91%), which we report transparently rather than gate the block-sharing decision on a single threshold.

</details>


### [78] [Fast Matrix Multiplication in fp8: Certified Coefficient Optimization and Measured Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26077)
*Xie, Shuxiao,Xie, Shuyang,Cao, Yuan,Ran, Dezhi,Yang, Wei,Xie, Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过系数泛函Φ在换基轨道上的最小化，为Strassen型fp8矩阵乘法实现在~70B LLM训练中提供了全局最优认证，实测减少10-55%过剩NLL，将实现选择从调参变为数学设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Strassen型矩阵乘法不同实现具有相同精确乘积和乘法次数，但在fp8下误差不同，如何选择最优实现缺乏全局认证；经典稳定性控制最坏情况l1增长，无法控制期望误差大小。

Method: 论文将矩阵乘法实现建模为Strassen型算法在换基轨道上的不同选择。每个实现关联一个系数泛函Φ，它是系数几何的标量摘要，在Hadamard流形上定义Kempf-Ness问题。通过计算矩映射的精确零点，得到Φ的全局最小值Φ_min=200/9，再利用de Groot对实秩7分解的分类结果，证明该最优性适用于所有精确实秩7的2x2分解。因此每个精确实秩7实现的Φ预测RMS常数至少为立方算法的5/3倍（固定噪声系数）。为连接理论与实际fp8误差，引入block-scaled e4m3模型，其中Φ作为相对期望均方误差的主导阶系数，从而可预测不同实现的误差排序。实验在融合block-scaled regime下进行排序和重基实验，并在来自四种架构族的真实matmul tile上验证Φ最优实现落在fp8低误差区域。最后在两个~70B模型上使用真实deep_gemm内核，对比Φ最优实现与经典Strassen，结果显示减少了10-55%的过剩NLL。整体上，论文将算法实现选择转化为数学认证的设计问题，提供了独立于低精度轴的可优化维度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Φ最小值", "value": "200/9", "setting": "在换基轨道上对系数泛函Φ的全局最小化（精确矩映射零）"}, {"metric": "Φ预测RMS常数下界", "value": "至少为立方算法的5/3倍", "setting": "所有精确实秩7实现，固定噪声系数"}, {"metric": "过剩NLL减少", "value": "10%到55%", "setting": "两个~70B模型在真实deep_gemm内核上，Φ最优实现相比经典Strassen"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 为每个实现引入系数泛函Φ作为系数几何的标量摘要，并在换基轨道上最小化；利用Kempf-Ness定理与Hadamard流形上的矩映射零，精确求解Φ最小值=200/9，并借助de Groot分类证明所有精确实秩7的2x2分解的全局最优性；提出显式block-scaled e4m3模型，其中Φ是相对期望均方误差的主导系数；在真实matmul tile和~70B模型deep_gemm内核上验证：Φ最优实现降低经典Strassen相对于干净模型的过剩NLL 10-55%

Abstract: A Strassen-type algorithm has many realizations with the same exact product and multiplication count yet different fp8 error because basis changes reshape coefficient geometry, posing the question of which to run. No current account settles this: classical stability controls worst-case $\ell_1$ growth, not the expected-error magnitude, and the Dumas--Pernet--Sedoglavic optimizer could only be called probably optimal, its global optimality unproved. To settle this, we attach to each realization a coefficient functional $\Phi$, a scalar summary of its coefficient geometry, which we minimize over the change-of-basis orbit. This Kempf--Ness problem on a Hadamard manifold lets us certify the global $\Phi$ optimum rather than merely search for it: an exact moment-map zero fixes $\Phi_{\min} = 200/9$, and de Groote's classification extends that optimality to every exact real rank-7 $2\times2$ decomposition. Every exact real rank-7 realization therefore has a $\Phi$-predicted RMS constant at least $5/3$ times that of the cubic algorithm, at fixed noise coefficient. We then introduce an explicit block-scaled e4m3 model in which $\Phi$ is the leading-order coefficient of relative expected mean-squared error, and we test the resulting $\Phi$-predicted ordering against realized fp8 error. Ordering and re-basing experiments support that prediction within tested fused block-scaled regimes, and on real matmul tiles from four architecture families the $\Phi$-optimal realization falls in the fp8 low-error region. Across two $\sim$70B models on real deep_gemm kernels, the same realization removes 10 to 55% of classic Strassen's excess NLL over the clean model. Algorithm realization thus becomes a mathematically certified design problem rather than a tuning choice: an independent low-precision axis with a global $\Phi$ optimum and measured fp8 relevance.

</details>


### [79] [Extending FunctionGemma for Practical On-Device Mobile Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25373)
*Rezagholizadeh, Ali,Samiee, Soheila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将FunctionGemma 270M-it扩展为实用的Android端侧函数调用模型：构建约9,500条、schema校验、覆盖15类设备控制的合成数据集MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED，用TRL监督微调（completion-only loss）训练扩展专项模型与联合训练的组合模型。在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上端到端准确率由基座29.3%、Google Mobile-Actions变体17.2%提升至76.5%；组合模型在MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE上达82.3%（Google专项模型为90.3%），以8.0个百分点换取类别覆盖翻倍。论文开源数据集、模型、可复现管线与Android demo，主打低延迟、隐私保护的端侧本地函数调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数调用数据集与模型多面向Web API或狭窄的移动动作目录，缺少覆盖实用Android设备控制场景的端侧小模型函数调用能力，难以在低延迟、隐私保护的前提下把自然语言映射为本地系统动作。

Method: 输入为移动端设备控制场景下的自然语言指令（连同函数调用schema）。基座为FunctionGemma 270M-it。训练路径：使用TRL库进行监督微调，损失仅在completion部分计算（completion-only loss），数据来自作者构建的MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED——约9,500条经schema校验的合成对话，覆盖十五类设备控制动作，包括消息、电话、相机/截图、亮度控制、设备状态查询、手电筒控制与应用管理。由此得到扩展专项模型；另一路将该数据与Google的MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE联合训练得到组合模型，用于同时保留原始动作目录与新增类别覆盖。推理路径为端侧本地执行：模型接收用户自然语言与可用函数定义，生成符合schema的函数调用（动作名与参数），由Android端解析并执行对应系统操作，因此无需网络往返，面向低延迟与隐私保护。评测为端到端准确率：在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上，扩展专项模型达76.5%，显著高于基座的29.3%与Google Mobile-Actions变体的17.2%；组合模型在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上保持76.5%，在MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE上为82.3%，相较Google专项模型的90.3%下降8.0个百分点，体现覆盖类别翻倍与既有能力保持之间的权衡。论文同时释放数据集、微调模型、可复现训练/评测管线与Android demo。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "76.5%", "setting": "MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED评测集，扩展专项模型（270M，completion-only loss监督微调）"}, {"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "29.3%", "setting": "MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED评测集，FunctionGemma 270M-it基座模型"}, {"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "17.2%", "setting": "MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED评测集，Google Mobile-Actions变体"}, {"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "76.5%", "setting": "MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED评测集，与MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE联合训练的组合模型"}, {"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "82.3%", "setting": "MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE评测集，组合模型（对比Google Mobile-Actions专项模型的90.3%）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED：约9,500条对话、schema校验的合成数据集，覆盖15类设备控制（消息、电话、相机/截图、亮度、设备状态查询、手电筒、应用管理等）；在FunctionGemma 270M-it上使用TRL监督微调并采用completion-only loss，得到扩展的专项模型；与Google的MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE联合训练得到组合模型，在覆盖范围与原有能力之间做权衡分析；释放数据集、微调模型、可复现训练/评测管线与Android demo

Abstract: On-device assistants require function-calling models that map natural language to local system actions, but existing resources emphasize web APIs or narrow mobile-action catalogs. We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>
